                        met["ridge_penalty_weight"] = args.ridge_penalty_weight
                        met["landcover_penalty_weight"] = args.landcover_penalty_weight
                        met["official_hazard_weight"] = args.official_hazard_weight
                    if m == "A*(turn-aware)":
                        met["astar_heuristic_weight"] = args.astar_heuristic_weight
                        met["turn_penalty_weight"] = args.turn_penalty_weight
                        met["ridge_penalty_weight"] = args.ridge_penalty_weight
                        met["landcover_penalty_weight"] = args.landcover_penalty_weight
                        met["official_hazard_weight"] = args.official_hazard_weight
                    met["time_s"] = round({
                        "VIN-Grad": t_vin,
                        "PPO": t_ppo,
                        "Theta*(experimental)": t_theta,
                        "A*(baseline)": t_astar,
                        "A*(weighted)": t_astar,
                        "A*(turn-aware)": t_astar,
                        TOWER_GRAPH_METHOD: t_tower_graph,
                    }.get(m, experimental_neighbor_results.get(m, {}).get("time_s", 0.0)), 2)
                    if m == TOWER_GRAPH_METHOD and experimental_tower_graph_results:
                        met["search_objective_model"] = "candidate_tower_graph_screening"
                        met["search_path_objective"] = {
                            "status": ("measured" if geo else
                                       "rejected_after_search" if experimental_tower_graph_results["path"]
                                       else "no_path"),
                            "unit": "cost_meter_plus_turn_penalty",
                            "tower_graph_parameters": {
                                key: value for key, value in experimental_tower_graph_results.items()
                                if key not in ("path", "time_s")
                            },
                            "objective_scope": (
                                "screening only; zero tower-site cost; optional parabolic DEM clearance "
                                "is not conductor or load-case certification"),
                        }
                        met["candidate_validation_status"] = (
                            "survived_validation" if geo else
                            "rejected_after_search" if experimental_tower_graph_results["path"]
                            else "no_path")
                    if m in experimental_neighbor_results:
                        experiment = experimental_neighbor_results[m]
                        search_cost = experiment["search_cost"]
                        met["search_path_objective"] = {
                            "status": ("measured" if math.isfinite(search_cost)
                                       else "invalid_path" if experiment["path"] else "no_path"),
                            "unit": "cost_meter",
                            "search_objective_cost_meter": search_cost if math.isfinite(search_cost) else None,
                            "raster_cost": (experiment["search_raster_cost"]
                                            if math.isfinite(experiment["search_raster_cost"])
                                            else None),
                            "soft_grade_penalty_cost_meter": (
                                experiment["search_grade_penalty_cost"]
                                if math.isfinite(experiment["search_grade_penalty_cost"])
                                else None),
                            "soft_grade_penalty_weight": experiment[
                                "grade_penalty_weight"],
                            "neighbor_count": len(experiment["neighbors"]),
                            "turn_penalty": 0.0,
                            "hard_ground_grade_limit": args.max_ground_grade,
                            "objective_scope": experiment["objective_scope"],
                        }
                        met["candidate_validation_status"] = (
                            "survived_validation" if geo else
                            "rejected_after_search" if experiment["path"] else "no_path")
                        met["search_objective_model"] = (
                            f"{len(experiment['neighbors'])}_neighbor_exact_raster_integral"
                            + ("_plus_endpoint_grade_penalty"
                               if experiment["grade_penalty_weight"] > 0.0 else ""))
                    if not station_task:
                        start_center = rowcol_to_geo(sr, sc, transform)
                        end_center = rowcol_to_geo(er, ec, transform)
                        met["start_input_to_grid_center_offset_m"] = round(
                            haversine_km(slon, slat, *start_center) * 1000.0, 2)
                        met["end_input_to_grid_center_offset_m"] = round(
                            haversine_km(elon, elat, *end_center) * 1000.0, 2)
                elif m in grade_reports:
                    met = {}
                else:
                    continue
                if m in grade_reports:
                    met.update(grade_reports[m])
                met.update(hard_violation_reports.get(m, {}))
                if m == TOWER_GRAPH_METHOD and experimental_tower_graph_results:
                    clearance_status = experimental_tower_graph_results[
                        "clearance_profile_status"]
                    met.update({
                        "candidate_validation_status": tower_candidate_validation_status(
                            geo, experimental_tower_graph_results["path"], clearance_status,
                            met.get("building_geometry_status")),
                        "clearance_profile_screening": experimental_tower_graph_results[
                            "clearance_profile_screening"],
                        "clearance_profile_status": clearance_status,
                        "clearance_profile_spans": experimental_tower_graph_results[
                            "clearance_profile_spans"],
                        "dem_vertical_datum": experimental_tower_graph_results[
                            "dem_vertical_datum"],
                    })
                if m in experimental_neighbor_results:
                    experiment = experimental_neighbor_results[m]
                    search_cost = experiment["search_cost"]
                    met["time_s"] = round(experiment["time_s"], 2)
                    met.setdefault("search_objective_model",
                                   f"{len(experiment['neighbors'])}_neighbor_exact_raster_integral")
                    met.setdefault("search_path_objective", {
                        "status": ("measured" if math.isfinite(search_cost)
                                   else "invalid_path" if experiment["path"] else "no_path"),
                        "unit": "cost_meter",
                        "search_objective_cost_meter": search_cost if math.isfinite(search_cost) else None,
                        "raster_cost": (experiment["search_raster_cost"]
                                        if math.isfinite(experiment["search_raster_cost"])
                                        else None),
                        "soft_grade_penalty_cost_meter": (
                            experiment["search_grade_penalty_cost"]
                            if math.isfinite(experiment["search_grade_penalty_cost"])
                            else None),
                        "soft_grade_penalty_weight": experiment[
                            "grade_penalty_weight"],
                        "neighbor_count": len(experiment["neighbors"]),
                        "turn_penalty": 0.0,
                        "hard_ground_grade_limit": args.max_ground_grade,
                        "objective_scope": experiment["objective_scope"],
                    })
                    met.setdefault("candidate_validation_status", (
                        "rejected_after_search" if experiment["path"] else "no_path"))
                    met.setdefault("search_objective_model", (
                        f"{len(experiment['neighbors'])}_neighbor_exact_raster_integral"
                        + ("_plus_endpoint_grade_penalty"
                           if experiment["grade_penalty_weight"] > 0.0 else "")))
                if geo and "hard_constraint_status" not in met:
                    met["hard_constraint_status"] = (
                        "pass" if met.get("hard_violations", 0) == 0 else "fail")
                met.update(station_offsets_by_method.get(m, {}))
                case_metrics[m] = met

            if (experimental_tower_graph_results
                    and TOWER_GRAPH_METHOD not in case_metrics):
                case_metrics[TOWER_GRAPH_METHOD] = {
                    "candidate_validation_status": tower_candidate_validation_status(
                        None, experimental_tower_graph_results["path"],
                        experimental_tower_graph_results["clearance_profile_status"],
                        None),
                    "clearance_profile_screening": experimental_tower_graph_results[
                        "clearance_profile_screening"],
                    "clearance_profile_status": experimental_tower_graph_results[
                        "clearance_profile_status"],
                    "clearance_profile_spans": experimental_tower_graph_results[
                        "clearance_profile_spans"],
                    "dem_vertical_datum": experimental_tower_graph_results[
                        "dem_vertical_datum"],
                }

            # 按固定优先规则筛选硬约束可行路线；实验 Pareto 候选不在选择优先表中。
            # 真实线仍只在规划选路完成后读取，用于事后评分。
            best_method, _, selection_rule = _select_final_route(
                methods, case_metrics,
                select_tower_graph=args.select_experimental_tower_graph)
            final_geo = methods.get(best_method) if best_method else None
            final_is_ai = False if best_method else None
            for method, candidate in sampled_pareto_by_method.items():
                geo = methods.get(method)
                canonical_geo = (json.dumps(
                    [[float(lon), float(lat)] for lon, lat in geo],
                    ensure_ascii=False, separators=(",", ":"), allow_nan=False)
                    if geo else None)
                candidate["post_validation_path_sha256"] = (
                    hashlib.sha256(canonical_geo.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
                    if canonical_geo else None)
                candidate["candidate_frozen_before_reference_read"] = True
            sampled_pareto_freeze_sha256 = None
            if args.experimental_sampled_pareto:
                final_dir = OUT / route_output_name(cname)
                final_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
                freeze_manifest = {
                    "scope": "finite_scalarization_samples_only",
                    "objective_vector_scope": "pre_smoothing_search_grid_paths",
                    "reference_geometry_used_for_planning": False,
                    "frozen_before_reference_read": True,
                    "candidates": [
                        {
                            "candidate_id": method,
                            "path_sha256": candidate[
                                "post_validation_path_sha256"],
                            "sampled_environmental_weights": candidate[
                                "sampled_environmental_weights"],
                            "objective_vector_on_search_grid": candidate[
                                "objective_vector_on_search_grid"],
                            "post_validation_status": hard_violation_reports.get(
                                method, {}).get("hard_constraint_status", "no_path"),
                        }
                        for method, candidate in sampled_pareto_by_method.items()
                    ],
                }
                freeze_bytes = json.dumps(
                    freeze_manifest, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2,
                    allow_nan=False).encode("utf-8")
                (final_dir / "sampled_pareto_freeze.json").write_bytes(freeze_bytes)
                sampled_pareto_freeze_sha256 = hashlib.sha256(freeze_bytes).hexdigest()
            osm_data_quality, missing_osm_layers = screening_data_quality(osm_layer_metadata)
            hazard_coverage_complete = (
                official_hazard_metadata.get("coverage_status") == "complete")
            missing_hazard_layers = (
                [] if args.official_hazard_weight == 0 or hazard_coverage_complete
                else ["official_hazards"])
            missing_screening_layers = missing_osm_layers + missing_hazard_layers
            if (args.population_weight > 0
                    and population_metadata.get("status") != "complete"):
                missing_screening_layers.append("population_raster_coverage")
            screening_quality = (
                "incomplete_screening_layers" if missing_screening_layers
                else "configured_layers_present_osm_coverage_unverified")
            hazard_route_exposure = route_hazard_exposure(
                final_geo, official_hazard_masks, transform, dem.shape)
            selected_metrics = case_metrics.get(best_method, {}) if best_method else {}

            real_evaluation_status = "not_requested"
            real_evaluation_methods = []
            if use_real:
                real_evaluation_status = "reference_missing"
                if global_real_exists:
                    real_geo, real_evaluation_status = load_case_reference(
                        args.real_shp, require_single_line=True)
                else:
                    try:
                        rp = resolve_real_path(
                            cname, args.real_dir,
                            include_global_mapping=allow_global_reference_mapping)
                    except AmbiguousReferenceError as exc:
                        print(f"  [warn] 目标线路参考文件匹配不唯一: {exc}")
                        real_geo, real_evaluation_status = None, "reference_ambiguous"
                    else:
                        real_geo, real_evaluation_status = load_case_reference(rp)
                if real_geo:
                    print(f"  [POST-EVAL] 加载真实线路: {len(real_geo)}条折线")
                    grid_corners = [transform * point for point in (
                        (0, 0), (W, 0), (W, H), (0, H))]
                    planning_bounds = (
                        min(point[0] for point in grid_corners),
                        min(point[1] for point in grid_corners),
                        max(point[0] for point in grid_corners),
                        max(point[1] for point in grid_corners),
                    )
                    for method, geo in methods.items():
                        if not geo or method not in case_metrics:
                            continue
                        dev = compute_deviation(
                            geo, real_geo, planning_bounds_wgs84=planning_bounds)
                        if not dev:
                            continue
                        metrics = case_metrics[method]
                        err = eval_planned_route_error(geo, dev)
                        if err:
                            metrics.update(err)
                        real_evaluation_methods.append(method)
                        if err:
                            similarity = err["route_similarity"]
                            symmetric = similarity["symmetric"]
                            coverage_100m = symmetric["coverage_f1_within_m"]["100"]
                            coverage_1km = symmetric["coverage_f1_within_m"]["1000"]
                            print(f"  [{method}] 较差方向P95={symmetric['p95_m']:.0f}m "
                                  f"采样最大偏移={symmetric['sampled_hausdorff_m']:.0f}m "
                                  f"100m覆盖F1={coverage_100m:.1%} "
                                  f"1km覆盖F1={coverage_1km:.1%} "
                                  f"长度差={similarity['length']['absolute_difference_km']:.2f}km")
                    real_evaluation_status = (
                        "comparison_completed" if real_evaluation_methods
                        else "reference_loaded_no_comparable_route")

            selected_metrics = case_metrics.get(best_method, {}) if best_method else {}
            planning_status = route_planning_status(
                final_geo, station_task, missing_screening_layers)
            case_metrics[FINAL_ROUTE_LABEL] = {
                "method": best_method,
                "selection_rule": selection_rule,
                "route_similarity_version": selected_metrics.get("route_similarity_version"),
                "route_similarity": selected_metrics.get("route_similarity"),
                "is_ai_method": final_is_ai,
                "real_evaluation_status": real_evaluation_status,
                "real_evaluation_methods": real_evaluation_methods,
                "planning_status": planning_status,
                **station_endpoint_properties(station_task),
                "hard_constraint_status": selected_metrics.get("hard_constraint_status", "no_path"),
                "hard_violations": selected_metrics.get("hard_violations"),
                "osm_data_quality": osm_data_quality,
                "screening_data_quality": screening_quality,
                "missing_osm_screening_layers": missing_osm_layers,
                "missing_screening_layers": missing_screening_layers,
                "official_hazard_metadata": official_hazard_metadata,
                "official_hazard_route_exposure": hazard_route_exposure,
                "constraint_layer_metadata": custom_constraint_metadata,
                "land_mask_metadata": land_mask_metadata,
                "length_km": round(path_length_km(final_geo), 3) if final_geo else None}
            case_metrics[FINAL_ROUTE_LABEL].update(selected_metrics)
            if final_geo:
                case_metrics[FINAL_ROUTE_LABEL].update(route_profile_metrics(final_geo, dem, transform))
            if best_method in grade_reports:
                case_metrics[FINAL_ROUTE_LABEL].update(grade_reports[best_method])
            case_metrics[FINAL_ROUTE_LABEL].update(station_offsets_by_method.get(best_method, {}))
            case_metrics[FINAL_ROUTE_LABEL].update({
                "settlement_weight": args.settlement_weight,
                "official_hazard_weight": args.official_hazard_weight,
                "turn_penalty_weight": args.turn_penalty_weight,
                "ridge_penalty_weight": args.ridge_penalty_weight,
                "landcover_penalty_weight": args.landcover_penalty_weight,
                "building_clearance_m": args.building_clearance_m,
                "grade_penalty_weight": args.grade_penalty_weight,
                "terrain_slope_weight": args.terrain_slope_weight,
                "roughness_weight": args.roughness_weight,
                "population_weight": args.population_weight,
                "population_layer": population_metadata,
                "population_data_coverage": route_population_coverage(
                    final_geo, population_valid_mask, transform, dem.shape),
                "max_ground_grade_ratio": args.max_ground_grade,
                "max_surface_slope_deg": args.max_surface_slope_deg,
                "max_terrain_elevation_m": args.max_terrain_elevation_m,
                "osm_layer_metadata": osm_layer_metadata,
                "voltage_rules_applied": False,
                "route_constraints_limitations": (
                    "Population raster, when enabled, is a modelled exposure proxy; no sensitive-target inventory, official protected-area boundaries, "
                    "conductor clearance, tower siting or span feasibility checked; administrative land polygons, when enabled, are a coarse land-area screen, not a surveyed shoreline"),
            })
            if station_task:
                start_station = station_task["from_station"]
                end_station = station_task["to_station"]
                station_measurements = station_elevation_metrics(station_task)
                for metrics in case_metrics.values():
                    if isinstance(metrics, dict):
                        metrics.update({
                            "route_id": cname,
                            "from_station_id": start_station["station_id"],
                            "from_station_name": start_station["name"],
                            "from_station_type": start_station["station_type"] or None,
                            "from_station_source": start_station["source"] or None,
                            "from_station_verified": start_station["verified"] or None,
                            "to_station_id": end_station["station_id"],
                            "to_station_name": end_station["name"],
                            "to_station_type": end_station["station_type"] or None,
                            "to_station_source": end_station["source"] or None,
                            "to_station_verified": end_station["verified"] or None,
                            **station_endpoint_properties(station_task),
                            "voltage_kv": station_task["voltage_kv"] or None,
                            **station_coordinate_crs_properties(station_task),
                            **station_measurements,
                            "settlement_weight": args.settlement_weight,
                            "ridge_penalty_weight": args.ridge_penalty_weight,
                            "landcover_penalty_weight": args.landcover_penalty_weight,
                            "building_clearance_m": args.building_clearance_m,
                            "grade_penalty_weight": args.grade_penalty_weight,
                            "terrain_slope_weight": args.terrain_slope_weight,
                            "roughness_weight": args.roughness_weight,
                            "population_weight": args.population_weight,
                            "population_layer": population_metadata,
                            "max_ground_grade_ratio": args.max_ground_grade,
                            "max_surface_slope_deg": args.max_surface_slope_deg,
                            "max_terrain_elevation_m": args.max_terrain_elevation_m,
                            "route_constraints_limitations": "Population raster, when enabled, is a modelled exposure proxy; no sensitive-target inventory, official protected-area layer, conductor clearance, tower siting or span feasibility checked",
                        })
            print(f"  [集成] 最终路线采用 {best_method} ({selection_rule})；线路相似度仅作事后指标")
            # 导出最终路线矢量 (GeoJSON)
            safe_now = route_output_name(cname)
            final_dir = OUT / safe_now
            final_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            try:
                if final_geo:
                    _fg = [{"type": "Feature",
                            "geometry": {"type": "LineString",
                                         "coordinates": geojson_coordinates(final_geo)},
                            "properties": {"case": cname, "method": best_method,
                                            "route_similarity_version": selected_metrics.get("route_similarity_version"),
                                            "route_similarity": selected_metrics.get("route_similarity"),
                                           "selection_rule": selection_rule,
                                           "planning_status": planning_status,
                                           **station_endpoint_properties(station_task),
                                           "hard_constraint_status": selected_metrics.get("hard_constraint_status", "no_path"),
                                           "hard_violations": selected_metrics.get("hard_violations"),
                                           "osm_data_quality": osm_data_quality,
                                           "screening_data_quality": screening_quality,
                                           "missing_osm_screening_layers": missing_osm_layers,
                                           "missing_screening_layers": missing_screening_layers,
                                           "official_hazard_metadata": official_hazard_metadata,
                                           "official_hazard_route_exposure": hazard_route_exposure,
                                           "constraint_layer_metadata": custom_constraint_metadata,
                                           "length_km": selected_metrics.get("length_km"),
                                           "start_input_to_grid_center_offset_m": selected_metrics.get("start_input_to_grid_center_offset_m"),
                                           "end_input_to_grid_center_offset_m": selected_metrics.get("end_input_to_grid_center_offset_m"),
                                           "mean_cost": selected_metrics.get("mean_cost"),
                                           "path_cost_integral": selected_metrics.get("path_cost_integral"),
                                           "astar_search_path_objective": selected_metrics.get("astar_search_path_objective"),
                                           "sinuosity": selected_metrics.get("sinuosity"),
                                           "cum_relief_m": selected_metrics.get("cum_relief_m"),
                                           "net_relief_m": selected_metrics.get("net_relief_m"),
                                           "from_station_id": station_task["from_station"]["station_id"] if station_task else None,
                                           "to_station_id": station_task["to_station"]["station_id"] if station_task else None,
                                           "from_station_name": station_task["from_station"]["name"] if station_task else None,
                                           "to_station_name": station_task["to_station"]["name"] if station_task else None,
                                           **station_coordinate_crs_properties(station_task),
                                           "from_station_source": station_task["from_station"]["source"] or None if station_task else None,
                                           "from_station_verified": station_task["from_station"]["verified"] or None if station_task else None,
                                           "to_station_source": station_task["to_station"]["source"] or None if station_task else None,
                                           "to_station_verified": station_task["to_station"]["verified"] or None if station_task else None,
                                           **station_endpoint_properties(station_task),
                                           "voltage_kv": station_task["voltage_kv"] or None if station_task else None,
                                           **station_measurements,
                                           "voltage_rules_applied": False,
                                           "settlement_weight": args.settlement_weight,
                                           "official_hazard_weight": args.official_hazard_weight,
                                           "turn_penalty_weight": args.turn_penalty_weight,
                                           "ridge_penalty_weight": args.ridge_penalty_weight,
                                           "landcover_penalty_weight": args.landcover_penalty_weight,
                                           "building_clearance_m": args.building_clearance_m,
                                           "grade_penalty_weight": args.grade_penalty_weight,
                                           "terrain_slope_weight": args.terrain_slope_weight,
                                           "roughness_weight": args.roughness_weight,
                                            "population_weight": args.population_weight,
                                            "population_layer": population_metadata,
                                            "population_data_coverage": route_population_coverage(
                                                final_geo, population_valid_mask, transform, dem.shape),
                                           "max_ground_grade_ratio": args.max_ground_grade,
                                           "max_surface_slope_deg": args.max_surface_slope_deg,
                                           "max_terrain_elevation_m": args.max_terrain_elevation_m,
                                           "osm_layer_metadata": osm_layer_metadata,
                                           "route_constraints_limitations": (
                                                "Population raster, when enabled, is a modelled exposure proxy; no sensitive-target inventory, official protected-area boundaries, "
                                               "conductor clearance, tower siting or span feasibility checked"),
                                           **grade_reports.get(best_method, {}),
                                           **station_offsets_by_method.get(best_method, {}),
                                           **(route_profile_metrics(final_geo, dem, transform) if final_geo else {})}}]
                    with open(final_dir / "final_route.geojson", "w", encoding="utf-8") as _ff:
                        json.dump({"type": "FeatureCollection", "features": _fg}, _ff, ensure_ascii=False)
                    if final_geo:
                        write_elevation_profile_csv(final_geo, dem, transform, final_dir / "elevation_profile.csv")
                if args.experimental_sampled_pareto:
                    pareto_manifest = {
                        "scope": "finite_scalarization_samples_only",
                        "objective_vector_scope": "pre_smoothing_search_grid_paths",
                        "reference_geometry_used_for_planning": False,
                        "freeze_manifest": "sampled_pareto_freeze.json",
                        "freeze_manifest_sha256": sampled_pareto_freeze_sha256,
                        "candidates": [],
                    }
                    for method, candidate in sampled_pareto_by_method.items():
                        metrics = case_metrics.get(method, {})
                        suffix = method.rsplit(" ", 1)[-1]
                        pareto_manifest["candidates"].append({
                            "candidate_id": method,
                            "geojson": f"planned_route_sampled_pareto_{suffix}.geojson"
                            if methods.get(method) else None,
                            **candidate,
                            "post_validation_status": metrics.get(
                                "hard_constraint_status", "no_path"),
                            "hard_violations": metrics.get("hard_violations"),
                            "post_smoothing_path_cost_integral": metrics.get(
                                "path_cost_integral"),
                            "post_validation_path_sha256": candidate.get(
                                "post_validation_path_sha256"),
                            "candidate_frozen_before_reference_read": candidate.get(
                                "candidate_frozen_before_reference_read"),
                            "route_similarity_version": metrics.get(
                                "route_similarity_version"),
                            "route_similarity": metrics.get("route_similarity"),
                            "length_km": metrics.get("length_km"),
                        })
                    with open(final_dir / "sampled_pareto_candidates.json", "w",
                              encoding="utf-8") as _pf:
                        json.dump(pareto_manifest, _pf, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
            except Exception as exc:
                raise RuntimeError(f"最终路线必需文件写出失败: {exc}") from exc
            all_results.append({"case": cname, "metrics": case_metrics})
            # 增量写出真实对比 JSON: 任一走廊完成后即落盘, 即使后续走廊异常/SIGKILL 终止也能保留已完成结果
            try:
                _real_out = []
                for _rec in all_results:
                    _entry = {"case": _rec["case"]}
                    for _m, _met in _rec["metrics"].items():
                        if "route_similarity" in _met:
                            _entry[_m] = {
                                "method": _met.get("method", _m),
                                "length_km": _met.get("length_km"),
                                "route_similarity_version": _met.get("route_similarity_version"),
                                "route_similarity": _met["route_similarity"],
                            }
                    if _entry:
                        _real_out.append(_entry)
                with open(OUT / "real_comparison_metrics.json", "w", encoding="utf-8") as _f:
                    json.dump(_real_out, _f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
            except Exception:
                pass
            # 逐走廊绘制规划路线与参考线对比图并导出矢量。
            safe = route_output_name(cname)
            cdir = OUT / safe
            cdir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            plot_comparison(dem, transform, methods, start_ll, end_ll,
                            cdir / f"path_comparison_{safe}.png",
                            title=f"规划路线与参考线路对比 - {cname}", real_ll=real_geo,
                            hazard_masks=official_hazard_masks)
            if args.experimental_sampled_pareto:
                sampled_methods = {
                    name: methods[name] for name in sampled_pareto_by_method
                    if methods.get(name)
                }
                if sampled_methods:
                    plot_comparison(
                        dem, transform, sampled_methods, start_ll, end_ll,
                        cdir / f"sampled_pareto_comparison_{safe}.png",
                        title=f"启发式多目标候选（非工程定线）- {cname}",
                        real_ll=real_geo, hazard_masks=official_hazard_masks,
                    )
            # 逐用例导出矢量与坐标(便于复用/二次绘图)
            for m, geo in methods.items():
                if m.startswith("Sampled Pareto "):
                    tag = f"sampled_pareto_{m.rsplit(' ', 1)[-1]}"
                else:
                    tag = m.split("(")[0].strip().lower().replace("*", "astar").replace("-", "")
                export_route(geo, crs, cdir / f"planned_route_{tag}.shp",
                             cdir / f"planned_route_{tag}.geojson")
            # 导出真实/参考线路(便于下游复用与二次对比)
            if real_geo:
                export_route(real_geo, crs, cdir / "real_route.shp", cdir / "real_route.geojson")
            paths_json = {m: [[round(lon, 6), round(lat, 6)] for lon, lat in geo]
                          for m, geo in methods.items() if geo}
            with open(cdir / "paths.json", "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                json.dump(paths_json, f, ensure_ascii=False)

            # 仅首个用例额外交互兼容产物(单图 + 值图 + 主方法指标)
            OUT.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            if not main_done:
                # 立即置位, 防止任一子步骤异常被外层 per-corridor except 捕获后 main_done 未设置,
                # 导致后续走廊重新进入本块并错误覆盖根目录产物。
                main_done = True
                try:
                    plot_comparison(dem, transform, methods, start_ll, end_ll,
                                    OUT / "path_comparison.png", title=f"规划路线与参考线路对比 - {cname}",
                                    real_ll=real_geo, hazard_masks=official_hazard_masks)
                except Exception as _pe:
                    print(f"[WARN] 主对比图绘制失败(跳过, 不影响本走廊其余产出): {_pe}")
                if "VIN-Grad" in methods and HAS_TORCH and V_main is not None:
                    # 复用主流程已计算的 V（避免重复 80 步 Bellman 训练）
                    try:
                        plot_value_map(V_main, transform, methods["VIN-Grad"], start_ll, end_ll, OUT / "value_map.png")
                    except Exception as _ve:
                        print(f"[WARN] 值图绘制失败(跳过): {_ve}")
                if "VIN-Grad" in case_metrics:
                    try:
                        with open(OUT / "metrics.json", "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                            json.dump(case_metrics["VIN-Grad"], f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
                    except Exception as _me:
                        print(f"[WARN] metrics.json 写出失败(跳过): {_me}")
        except Exception as _exc:
            import traceback as _tb
            _tb.print_exc()
            print(f"[WARN] 用例 {cname} 处理失败, 跳过此用例: {_exc}")
            failed_cases.append({"case": cname, "error": format_case_error(_exc)})
        finally:
            # Drop per-case raster/vector references before loading the next window.
            dem = population_density = population_proxy = population_valid_mask = None
            population_unknown_mask = dem_valid_mask = None
            osm = terrain = stack = model_cost = cost = None
            slope = elev = building_mask = building_geometries = None
            custom_constraints = endpoint_blockers = endpoint_building_exceptions = None
            hard_mask = tower_span_mask = cost_grid = tower_span_cost_grid = None
            official_hazard_masks = methods = experimental_neighbor_results = None
            experimental_tower_graph_results = sampled_pareto_by_method = None
            real_geo = None
            import gc as _gc
            _gc.collect()
            if HAS_TORCH:
                try:
                    import torch as _torch
                    _torch.cuda.empty_cache()
                except Exception:
                    pass

    run_summary = {
        "scope": "per_case_planning_and_required_route_artifacts",
        **summarize_run_completion(len(cases), all_results, failed_cases),
        "real_evaluation_by_case": [
            {"case": record["case"],
             "status": record["metrics"].get(FINAL_ROUTE_LABEL, {}).get("real_evaluation_status", "not_recorded"),
             "methods": record["metrics"].get(FINAL_ROUTE_LABEL, {}).get("real_evaluation_methods", [])}
            for record in all_results
        ],
        "dem": str(dem_path),
        "max_side": args.max_side,
        "argv": sys.argv,
    }
    try:
        with open(OUT / "run_summary.json", "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            json.dump(run_summary, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
    except Exception as _exc:
        print(f"[WARN] 批次运行摘要写出失败: {_exc}")
    print(f"[批次状态] 应测 {len(cases)} 条，完成 {run_summary['completed_cases']} 条，"
          f"无可行路线 {len(run_summary['no_route_cases'])} 条，失败 {len(failed_cases)} 条")

    # 聚合对比图 + 指标表
    try:
        plot_algo_comparison(all_results, OUT / "algo_comparison.png")
        with open(OUT / "comparison_metrics.json", "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            json.dump(all_results, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
    except Exception as _exc2:
        import traceback as _tb2
        _tb2.print_exc()
        print(f"[WARN] 聚合对比图/指标写出失败(不影响各走廊已写出结果): {_exc2}")
    # CSV
    rows = []
    for rec in all_results:
        for m, met in rec["metrics"].items():
            row = {"case": rec["case"], "method": m, **met}
            if "route_similarity" in row:
                row["route_similarity_json"] = json.dumps(
                    row.pop("route_similarity"), ensure_ascii=False)
            rows.append(row)
    if rows:
        import csv
        # 用所有行的键并集作为表头, 避免不同方法(A*/VIN/PPO)或有无真实对比导致字段不一致
        all_keys = []
        for r in rows:
            for k in r.keys():
                if k not in all_keys:
                    all_keys.append(k)
        with open(OUT / "comparison_metrics.csv", "w", encoding="utf-8", newline="") as f:
            w = csv.DictWriter(f, fieldnames=all_keys)
            w.writeheader(); w.writerows(rows)

    # 参考线路空间相似度 JSON + 控制台摘要
    if use_real:
        real_out = []
        for rec in all_results:
            entry = {"case": rec["case"]}
            for m, met in rec["metrics"].items():
                if "route_similarity" in met:
                    entry[m] = {
                        "method": met.get("method", m),
                        "length_km": met.get("length_km"),
                        "route_similarity_version": met.get("route_similarity_version"),
                        "route_similarity": met["route_similarity"],
                    }
            if entry:
                real_out.append(entry)
        try:
            with open(OUT / "real_comparison_metrics.json", "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                json.dump(real_out, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
        except Exception:
            pass
        print("\n" + "=" * 70)
        print("  参考线路空间相似度 (双向米制偏移 + 线路覆盖；仅事后评估)")
        print("=" * 70)
        print(f"  {'走廊':16s} {'方法':16s} {'较差方向P95(m)':>14s} {'采样最大(m)':>12s} "
              f"{'F1@100m':>9s} {'F1@1km':>9s} {'长度差(km)':>11s}")
        candidate_methods = ["A*(baseline)", "A*(weighted)", "A*(turn-aware)",
                             TOWER_GRAPH_METHOD,
                             ASTAR_8_EXACT_METHOD, ASTAR_16_METHOD,
                             ASTAR_8_SHARED_METHOD, ASTAR_16_SHARED_METHOD,
                             "Theta*(experimental)", "VIN-Grad", "PPO"]
        failed_case_names = {item["case"] for item in failed_cases}
        for rec in all_results:
            if rec["case"] in failed_case_names or rec["metrics"].get(FINAL_ROUTE_LABEL, {}).get("planning_status") == "no_hard_constraint_compliant_route":
                continue
            for m in candidate_methods:
                met = rec["metrics"].get(m, {})
                similarity = met.get("route_similarity")
                if similarity:
                    symmetric = similarity["symmetric"]
                    print(f"  {rec['case']:16s} {m:16s} "
                          f"{symmetric['p95_m']:11.0f} "
                          f"{symmetric['sampled_hausdorff_m']:12.0f} "
                          f"{symmetric['coverage_f1_within_m']['100']:8.1%} "
                          f"{symmetric['coverage_f1_within_m']['1000']:8.1%} "
                          f"{similarity['length']['absolute_difference_km']:11.2f}")
        print("  说明：误差与覆盖率只作事后诊断，不设贴合率达标线，也不参与选路。")
        print("-" * 70)
        for m in candidate_methods:
            summary = method_visual_similarity_summary(
                all_results, m, expected_cases=len(cases),
                failed_case_names=failed_case_names,
            )
            p95 = summary["median_symmetric_p95_m"]
            max_offset = summary["median_sampled_hausdorff_m"]
            f1_100m = summary["median_coverage_f1_100m"]
            f1 = summary["median_coverage_f1_1km"]
            length_difference = summary["median_length_difference_pct"]
            p95_text = f"{p95:.1f}m" if p95 is not None else "NA"
            max_text = f"{max_offset:.1f}m" if max_offset is not None else "NA"
            f1_100m_text = f"{f1_100m:.1%}" if f1_100m is not None else "NA"
            f1_text = f"{f1:.1%}" if f1 is not None else "NA"
            length_text = f"{length_difference:.1f}%" if length_difference is not None else "NA"
            print(f"  {m:16s} 已测={summary['measured']:2d} 未测={summary['unmeasured']:2d} "
                  f"中位较差方向P95={p95_text} 中位最大偏移={max_text} "
                  f"中位F1@100m={f1_100m_text} 中位F1@1km={f1_text} "
                  f"中位长度差={length_text}")
        heuristic_selected = [r["case"] for r in all_results
                          if r["case"] not in failed_case_names
                          and r["metrics"].get(FINAL_ROUTE_LABEL, {}).get("planning_status")
                          != "no_hard_constraint_compliant_route"
                          if r["metrics"].get(FINAL_ROUTE_LABEL, {}).get("method")
                          in ("A*(baseline)", "A*(weighted)", "A*(turn-aware)")]
        if heuristic_selected:
            print(f"  启发式搜索生成最终路线的走廊: {heuristic_selected}")
        print("=" * 70)

    # 控制台摘要
    print("\n" + "=" * 70)
    print("  结果摘要（各走廊均值）")
    print("=" * 70)
    for m in ["A*(baseline)", "A*(weighted)", "A*(turn-aware)",
              ASTAR_8_EXACT_METHOD, ASTAR_16_METHOD,
              ASTAR_8_SHARED_METHOD, ASTAR_16_SHARED_METHOD,
              "Theta*(experimental)", "VIN-Grad", "PPO"]:
        method_metrics = _method_route_metrics(all_results, m)
        lens = [metrics["length_km"] for metrics in method_metrics]
        mc = [metrics["mean_cost"] for metrics in method_metrics]
        if lens:
            print(f"  {m:14s} 平均长度 {np.mean(lens):7.2f}km  平均代价 {np.mean(mc):.3f}")
    print(f"  总耗时: {time.time() - t0:.1f}s")
    print(f"  输出目录: {OUT}")
    print("=" * 70)
    try:
        provenance_path = write_run_provenance(
            run_provenance, OUT,
            (args.real_dir, Path(args.real_shp).parent if args.real_shp else None))
        print(f"[运行溯源] {provenance_path}")
    except Exception as exc:
        print(f"[WARN] 运行溯源写出失败: {exc}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

def cuda_is_usable(torch_module=None):
    module = torch if torch_module is None else torch_module
    if module is None:
        return False
    try:
        if not module.cuda.is_available():
            return False
        device_count = getattr(module.cuda, "device_count", None)
        return callable(device_count) and device_count() > 0
    except Exception:
        return False


HAS_CUDA = cuda_is_usable()

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""旧版输电走廊先验工具。

离线构造既有线路距离/方向场和区域密度场，供显式调用方实验使用。
当前 CLI 未导入本模块；规划路线不使用这些先验，也不读取待评估线路引导。
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import pickle
import threading
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np


def pickle_to_np(obj):
    """任意 Python 对象 -> np.uint8 数组(可存入 npz)。"""
    return np.frombuffer(pickle.dumps(obj, protocol=4), dtype=np.uint8)


def pickle_from_np(arr):
    """np.uint8 数组 -> 原对象。"""
    return pickle.loads(arr.tobytes())


def cache_load_real_geo(arr):
    """兼容新旧缓存格式读取 real_geo, 统一为 折线列表, 每条 = [(lon,lat),...]。

    旧格式: np.array([real_geo], dtype=object) 可能落成 (1,1,N,2) 数值数组 或 (1,n) 对象数组;
    新格式: pickle 字节。
    """
    if arr.dtype == object:
        if arr.ndim == 1:
            val = arr[0]
        else:
            val = arr[0]  # (1,1,N,2) -> (1,N,2); (1,n) -> (n,)
    else:
        try:
            val = pickle.loads(arr.tobytes())
        except Exception:
            val = arr
    if val is None:
        return None
    if isinstance(val, np.ndarray):
        val = val.tolist()
    if isinstance(val, tuple):
        val = [val]
    out = []
    for poly in val:
        if isinstance(poly, np.ndarray):
            poly = poly.tolist()
        out.append([(float(x), float(y)) for x, y in poly])
    return out

_network_lock = threading.Lock()
_NETWORK_CACHE = {}  # path -> (coords_tuple_list, geom_list)


def load_network_lines(shp_path):
    """加载 SHP 中全部 LineString(将 MultiLineString 拆开), 进程级缓存。"""
    with _network_lock:
        if shp_path in _NETWORK_CACHE:
            return _NETWORK_CACHE[shp_path]
    import geopandas as gpd
    gdf = gpd.read_file(shp_path)
    coords_list, geom_list = [], []
    for geom in gdf.geometry:
        if geom is None or geom.is_empty:
            continue
        if geom.geom_type == "LineString":
            coords_list.append(tuple(geom.coords))
            geom_list.append(geom)
        elif geom.geom_type == "MultiLineString":
            for sub in geom.geoms:
                coords_list.append(tuple(sub.coords))
                geom_list.append(sub)
    with _network_lock:
        _NETWORK_CACHE[shp_path] = (coords_list, geom_list)
    return coords_list, geom_list


def _norm(c):
    return tuple((round(float(x), 6), round(float(y), 6)) for x, y in c)


def match_exclude_index(real_geo_coords, coords_list):
    """按坐标序列精确匹配待规划线在全网中的索引(留一排除); 支持反向。"""
    target = _norm(real_geo_coords)
    rev = tuple(reversed(target))
    for i, c in enumerate(coords_list):
        nc = _norm(c)
        if nc == target or nc == rev:
            return i
    return None


def corridor_dist_band(coords_list, geom_list, exclude_idx, transform, H, W,
                       max_dist_m=12000.0):
    """栅格化除 exclude_idx 外全部线路, 返回(到最近线路米距离场, 方向场(r,c))。"""
    from rasterio.features import rasterize as rio_rasterize
    from shapely.geometry import mapping
    from scipy.ndimage import distance_transform_edt
    from .geometry import pixel_meters

    if exclude_idx is not None:
        geoms = [g for i, g in enumerate(geom_list) if i != exclude_idx]
    else:
        geoms = geom_list
    mask = rio_rasterize([(mapping(g), 1) for g in geoms], out_shape=(H, W),
                         transform=transform, fill=0, dtype="uint8", all_touched=False)
    dist_px, idx = distance_transform_edt(1 - mask, return_indices=True)
    lat0 = transform.f + transform.e * (H / 2.0)
    pm = pixel_meters(transform, lat0)
    dist_m = np.minimum(dist_px * pm, max_dist_m).astype(np.float64)
    ir, ic = idx
    rr = np.arange(H)[:, None]
    cc = np.arange(W)[None, :]
    ar = (ir - rr).astype(np.float64)
    ac = (ic - cc).astype(np.float64)
    mag = np.hypot(ar, ac) + 1e-8
    ar /= mag
    ac /= mag
    return dist_m, (ar, ac), pm


def regional_density_band(geom_list, exclude_idx, transform, H, W, sigma_px=12.0):
    """区域走廊密度: 栅格化既有线路(留一)后高斯平滑, 归一化到 [0,1]。

    sigma_px 建议 8~25 像元(对应约 1.5~5km @150m/px), 表达'区域走廊偏好'。
    """
    from rasterio.features import rasterize as rio_rasterize
    from shapely.geometry import mapping
    from scipy.ndimage import gaussian_filter

    geoms = [g for i, g in enumerate(geom_list) if i != exclude_idx] if exclude_idx is not None else geom_list
    mask = rio_rasterize([(mapping(g), 1) for g in geoms], out_shape=(H, W),
                         transform=transform, fill=0, dtype="float32", all_touched=False)
    dens = gaussian_filter(mask, sigma=sigma_px)
    mx = dens.max()
    if mx <= 0:
        return np.zeros((H, W), np.float32)
    return (dens / mx).astype(np.float32)

def corridor_orientation_band(geom_list, exclude_idx, transform, H, W, win=5):
    """每像元'最近既有线路段'的局部方向(弧度, 模π∈[0,π))。

    思路: 线像元上按窗口PCA取主轴方向, 再用距离变换把方向传播到全图。
    无线路像元(孤立区)返回 NaN。
    """
    from rasterio.features import rasterize as rio_rasterize
    from shapely.geometry import mapping
    from scipy.ndimage import distance_transform_edt
    from numpy.linalg import eig
    import math

    geoms = [g for i, g in enumerate(geom_list) if i != exclude_idx] if exclude_idx is not None else geom_list
    mask = rio_rasterize([(mapping(g), 1) for g in geoms], out_shape=(H, W),
                         transform=transform, fill=0, dtype="uint8", all_touched=False)
    orient = np.full((H, W), np.nan, np.float64)
    ys, xs = np.nonzero(mask)
    w = max(win, 3)
    for y, x in zip(ys, xs):
        y0, y1 = max(0, y - w), min(H, y + w + 1)
        x0, x1 = max(0, x - w), min(W, x + w + 1)
        sub = mask[y0:y1, x0:x1]
        pts = np.argwhere(sub > 0).astype(np.float64) + np.array([y0, x0])
        if len(pts) < 3:
            orient[y, x] = 0.0
            continue
        mu = pts.mean(axis=0)
        cov = (pts - mu).T @ (pts - mu) / len(pts)
        wv, v = eig(cov)
        ang = math.atan2(v[1, int(np.argmax(wv))], v[0, int(np.argmax(wv))])
        orient[y, x] = ang % math.pi
    _, idx = distance_transform_edt(1 - mask, return_indices=True)
    ir, ic = idx
    return orient[ir, ic]
# ===== transmission_path_planner/cost.py =====
# transmission_path_planner/cost.py
"""26 维特征栈、CostUNet 建设代价面预测、启发式代价。"""

from .config import *
from .geometry import *
from .data import *

def _minmax01(band):
    b = np.asarray(band, np.float32)
    bmin, bmax = np.nanmin(b), np.nanmax(b)
    if not np.isfinite(bmin) or not np.isfinite(bmax) or (bmax - bmin) < 1e-9:
        return np.zeros_like(b)
    return np.clip((b - bmin) / (bmax - bmin), 0, 1).astype(np.float32)


def build_feature_stack(dem, terrain, osm, dist_existing=None):
    H, W = dem.shape
    stack = np.zeros((H, W, N_FEATURES), np.float32)
    # 13 维地形
    stack[:, :, 0] = _minmax01(terrain["elevation"])
    stack[:, :, 1] = _minmax01(terrain["slope"])
    stack[:, :, 2] = (terrain["aspect_cos"] * 0.5 + 0.5).astype(np.float32)
    stack[:, :, 3] = (terrain["aspect_sin"] * 0.5 + 0.5).astype(np.float32)
    stack[:, :, 4] = _minmax01(terrain["tri"])
    stack[:, :, 5] = _minmax01(terrain["tpi_100"])
    stack[:, :, 6] = _minmax01(terrain["tpi_300"])
    stack[:, :, 7] = _minmax01(terrain["tpi_900"])
    stack[:, :, 8] = _minmax01(np.abs(terrain["profile_curvature"]))
    stack[:, :, 9] = _minmax01(np.abs(terrain["plan_curvature"]))
    stack[:, :, 10] = _minmax01(terrain["roughness_3"])
    stack[:, :, 11] = _minmax01(terrain["roughness_9"])
    stack[:, :, 12] = _minmax01(terrain["roughness_27"])
    # 13-17 距离波段（归一化到 0-1）
    stack[:, :, 13] = np.clip(osm["dist_road"] / 5000.0, 0, 1)
    stack[:, :, 14] = np.clip(osm["dist_water"] / 5000.0, 0, 1)
    if dist_existing is not None:
        stack[:, :, 15] = np.clip(dist_existing / 10000.0, 0, 1)
    stack[:, :, 16] = np.clip(osm["dist_railway"] / 5000.0, 0, 1)
    stack[:, :, 17] = np.clip(osm["dist_fault"] / 10000.0, 0, 1)
    # 18-20 分类/密度/植被
    stack[:, :, 18] = osm["landuse_code"].astype(np.float32) / 8.0
    stack[:, :, 19] = np.clip(osm["building_density"], 0, 1)
    stack[:, :, 20] = np.clip(osm["vegetation_height"] / 30.0, 0, 1)
    # 21-25 风险
    for i, name in enumerate(["typhoon_risk", "seismic_risk", "landslide_risk", "ice_cover_risk", "lightning_risk"]):
        stack[:, :, 21 + i] = np.clip(osm[name], 0, 1).astype(np.float32)
    return stack


if HAS_TORCH:
    DEVICE = torch.device("cuda" if HAS_CUDA else "cpu")
    if DEVICE.type == "cuda":
        print(f"  [GPU] 使用 CUDA 设备: {torch.cuda.get_device_name(0)}")
    class DoubleConv(nn.Module):
        def __init__(self, in_ch, out_ch, dropout=0.0):
            super().__init__()
            self.conv = nn.Sequential(
                nn.Conv2d(in_ch, out_ch, 3, padding=1, bias=False),
                nn.BatchNorm2d(out_ch), nn.ReLU(inplace=True),
                nn.Conv2d(out_ch, out_ch, 3, padding=1, bias=False),
                nn.BatchNorm2d(out_ch), nn.ReLU(inplace=True))
            self.dropout = nn.Dropout2d(dropout) if dropout > 0 else nn.Identity()

        def forward(self, x):
            return self.dropout(self.conv(x))

    class ResidualBlock(nn.Module):
        def __init__(self, channels, dropout=0.0):
            super().__init__()
            self.conv1 = nn.Conv2d(channels, channels, 3, padding=1, bias=False)
            self.bn1 = nn.BatchNorm2d(channels)
            self.conv2 = nn.Conv2d(channels, channels, 3, padding=1, bias=False)
            self.bn2 = nn.BatchNorm2d(channels)
            self.dropout = nn.Dropout2d(dropout) if dropout > 0 else nn.Identity()

        def forward(self, x):
            out = F.relu(self.bn1(self.conv1(x)))
            out = self.dropout(out)
            out = self.bn2(self.conv2(out))
            return F.relu(out + x)

    class CostUNet(nn.Module):
        def __init__(self, n_features=N_FEATURES, enc=None, dec=None, bn=512, dropout=0.1):
            super().__init__()
            enc = enc or [32, 64, 128, 256, 512]
            dec = dec or [256, 128, 64, 32]
            self.encoder_blocks = nn.ModuleList()
            in_ch = n_features
            for o in enc:
                self.encoder_blocks.append(DoubleConv(in_ch, o, dropout))
                in_ch = o
            self.bottleneck = nn.Sequential(
                DoubleConv(enc[-1], bn, dropout),
                ResidualBlock(bn, dropout), ResidualBlock(bn, dropout))
            self.decoder_blocks = nn.ModuleList()
            self.up_convs = nn.ModuleList()
            for i, o in enumerate(dec):
                sk = enc[-(i + 1)] if i < len(enc) else enc[-1]
                self.up_convs.append(nn.ConvTranspose2d(in_ch, o, 2, stride=2))
                self.decoder_blocks.append(DoubleConv(o + sk, o, dropout))
                in_ch = o
            self.final_conv = nn.Sequential(
                nn.Conv2d(dec[-1], 16, 3, padding=1), nn.ReLU(inplace=True),
                nn.Conv2d(16, 1, 1), nn.Sigmoid())

        def forward(self, x):
            inp = x.shape[2:]
            skips = []
            for e in self.encoder_blocks:
                x = e(x); skips.append(x); x = F.max_pool2d(x, 2)
            x = self.bottleneck(x)
            for i, (up, d) in enumerate(zip(self.up_convs, self.decoder_blocks)):
                x = up(x); sk = skips[-(i + 1)]
                if x.shape[2:] != sk.shape[2:]:
                    x = F.interpolate(x, size=sk.shape[2:], mode="bilinear", align_corners=True)
                x = d(torch.cat([x, sk], 1))
            x = self.final_conv(x)
            if x.shape[2:] != inp:
                x = F.interpolate(x, size=inp, mode="bilinear", align_corners=True)
            return x

        def predict(self, stack):
            self.eval()
            H, W, C = stack.shape
            x = torch.from_numpy(np.ascontiguousarray(stack)).permute(2, 0, 1).unsqueeze(0).float().to(DEVICE)
            if H <= 512 and W <= 512:
                with torch.no_grad():
                    return self.forward(x).squeeze().cpu().numpy().astype(np.float32)
            ps, ov = 256, 32
            res = np.zeros((H, W), np.float32)
            w = np.zeros((H, W), np.float32)
            for r in range(0, H, ps - ov):
                for c in range(0, W, ps - ov):
                    re = min(r + ps, H); ce = min(c + ps, W)
                    r0 = max(0, re - ps); c0 = max(0, ce - ps)
                    pt = x[:, :, r0:re, c0:ce].to(DEVICE)
                    with torch.no_grad():
                        p = self.forward(pt).squeeze().cpu().numpy().astype(np.float32)
                    res[r0:re, c0:ce] += p
                    w[r0:re, c0:ce] += 1.0
            return (res / np.clip(w, 1e-6, None)).astype(np.float32)


def load_costunet():
    for p in MODEL_WEIGHTS:
        if p.exists():
            try:
                try:
                    ckpt = torch.load(p, map_location="cpu", weights_only=True)
                except Exception:
                    ckpt = torch.load(p, map_location="cpu", weights_only=False)
                sd = ckpt["state_dict"] if isinstance(ckpt, dict) and "state_dict" in ckpt else ckpt
                model = CostUNet(n_features=N_FEATURES)
                model.load_state_dict(sd)
                model.to(DEVICE)
                model.eval()
                print(f"  [AI] 已加载 CostUNet 权重: {p.name}")
                return model
            except Exception as ex:
                print(f"  [warn] 加载 {p.name} 失败: {ex}")
    return None


def heuristic_cost(dem, terrain):
    slope_n = _minmax01(terrain["slope"])
    rough_n = np.clip(_minmax01(terrain["roughness_27"]), 0, 1)
    return np.clip(0.65 * slope_n ** 1.5 + 0.20 * rough_n, 0, 1).astype(np.float32)


def build_route_cost(terrain, osm, settlement_weight=2.0, model_cost=None,
                     ridge_penalty_weight=0.10,
                     landcover_penalty_weight=0.0,
                     official_hazard_masks=None,
                     official_hazard_weight=0.0,
                     terrain_slope_weight=0.75,
                     roughness_weight=0.25,
                     population_proxy=None,
                     population_weight=0.0):
    """Build an interpretable screening cost with a soft built-up proxy.

    The proxy is the maximum of mapped-building occupancy fraction, mapped
    residential coverage, and land-use code 4 (residential/retail/commercial/
    urban). Taking the maximum combines overlapping OSM signals without
    double-counting them. Missing bands contribute no proxy; their availability
    must be read from OSM layer metadata. A nonzero land-cover weight adds a
    separate soft cost to mapped forest and wetland codes; missing land use
    remains neutral. Official hazard masks are unioned before a shared soft
    penalty is applied, so overlapping hazard categories are not double-counted.
    None of these screening costs is a hard exclusion or engineering standard.
    """
    slope = np.nan_to_num(terrain["slope"], nan=0.0, posinf=90.0, neginf=0.0)
    roughness = np.nan_to_num(terrain["roughness_27"], nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    terrain_cost = max(float(terrain_slope_weight), 0.0) * np.clip(slope / 45.0, 0.0, 1.0) ** 1.5
    terrain_cost += max(float(roughness_weight), 0.0) * np.clip(roughness / 100.0, 0.0, 1.0)
    # Positive TPI marks terrain above its local 300 m neighborhood; a weak
    # penalty prefers lower saddles over exposed crests without using route data.
    tpi_300 = np.broadcast_to(np.asarray(
        terrain.get("tpi_300", np.zeros_like(terrain_cost)), dtype=np.float32),
        terrain_cost.shape)
    ridge_proxy = np.clip(
        np.nan_to_num(tpi_300, nan=0.0, posinf=200.0, neginf=0.0) / 200.0,
        0.0, 1.0,
    )
    terrain_cost += max(float(ridge_penalty_weight), 0.0) * ridge_proxy

    density = np.broadcast_to(np.asarray(
        osm.get("building_density", np.zeros_like(terrain_cost)), dtype=np.float32),
        terrain_cost.shape)
    density = np.nan_to_num(density, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    building_proxy = np.clip(density, 0.0, 1.0)
    residential = np.broadcast_to(np.asarray(
        osm.get("residential_landuse_mask", np.zeros_like(terrain_cost)), dtype=np.float32),
        terrain_cost.shape)
    residential_proxy = np.clip(np.nan_to_num(residential, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0), 0.0, 1.0)
    landuse_code = np.broadcast_to(np.asarray(
        osm.get("landuse_code", np.zeros_like(terrain_cost)), dtype=np.float32),
        terrain_cost.shape)
    landuse_code = np.nan_to_num(landuse_code, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    builtup_proxy = (landuse_code == 4.0).astype(np.float32)
    settlement_proxy = np.maximum(np.maximum(building_proxy, residential_proxy), builtup_proxy)
    landcover_proxy = np.isin(landuse_code, (1.0, 7.0)).astype(np.float32)

    hazard_proxy = np.zeros_like(terrain_cost)
    if official_hazard_masks:
        for mask in official_hazard_masks.values():
            values = np.broadcast_to(np.asarray(mask, dtype=np.float32), terrain_cost.shape)
            hazard_proxy = np.maximum(
                hazard_proxy,
                np.clip(np.nan_to_num(values, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0), 0.0, 1.0),
            )

    if model_cost is not None:
        model = np.nan_to_num(model_cost, nan=0.0, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)
        terrain_cost = 0.5 * terrain_cost + 0.5 * np.clip(model, 0.0, 1.0)
    cost = (
        terrain_cost
        + max(float(settlement_weight), 0.0) * settlement_proxy
        + max(float(landcover_penalty_weight), 0.0) * landcover_proxy
        + max(float(official_hazard_weight), 0.0) * hazard_proxy
    ).astype(np.float32)
    population_weight = float(population_weight)
    if not np.isfinite(population_weight) or population_weight < 0:
        raise ValueError("population_weight must be finite and non-negative")
    if population_weight > 0:
        if population_proxy is None:
            raise ValueError("population_proxy is required when population_weight > 0")
        population_proxy = np.asarray(population_proxy, dtype=np.float32)
        if population_proxy.shape != cost.shape:
            raise ValueError("population_proxy shape must match route cost grid")
        if (not np.isfinite(population_proxy).all()
                or np.any(population_proxy < 0) or np.any(population_proxy > 1)):
            raise ValueError("population_proxy must contain finite values in [0, 1]")
        cost += population_weight * population_proxy
    return cost
# ===== transmission_path_planner/data.py =====
# transmission_path_planner/data.py
"""DEM 读取、地形因子计算、山体阴影。"""

from .config import *
from .geometry import *

def find_dem():
    for p in DEM_CANDIDATES:
        if p.exists():
            return p
    return None


POPULATION_DENSITY_NORMALIZATION_PEOPLE_PER_KM2 = 10000.0


def _matching_population_stac_metadata(population_path):
    source_name = Path(population_path).name
    for metadata_path in Path(population_path).parent.glob("stac_item*.json"):
        try:
            item = json.loads(metadata_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        except (OSError, ValueError):
            continue
        href = item.get("assets", {}).get("data", {}).get("href", "")
        asset_name = href.split("?", 1)[0].rsplit("/", 1)[-1]
        if asset_name == source_name:
            return item, metadata_path
    return {}, None


def load_population_density_window(population_path, shape, transform, crs,
                                   required_mask=None):
    """Warp people-per-cell counts to the DEM grid as people/km².

    Population pixels are converted using WGS84 ellipsoidal pixel areas before
    area-weighted alignment. Missing population data remains NaN and is
    reported in metadata and remains NaN, including wherever ``required_mask`` is true.
    """
    if not HAS_RASTERIO:
        raise RuntimeError("人口栅格需要 rasterio；请安装 rasterio 后重试")

    from pyproj import Geod
    from rasterio.enums import Resampling
    from rasterio.warp import reproject
    from rasterio.windows import Window, from_bounds

    required = (np.ones(shape, dtype=bool) if required_mask is None
                else np.asarray(required_mask, dtype=bool))
    if required.shape != tuple(shape):
        raise ValueError("required_mask shape must match population destination grid")

    with rasterio.open(population_path) as src:
        if src.count != 1:
            raise ValueError("人口栅格必须是单波段")
        if src.crs is None or src.crs != rasterio.crs.CRS.from_epsg(4326):
            raise ValueError("人口栅格必须使用 EPSG:4326")
        if (src.transform.b != 0 or src.transform.d != 0
                or src.transform.a <= 0 or src.transform.e >= 0):
            raise ValueError("人口栅格必须是北向上的 EPSG:4326 网格")
        source_tags = src.tags()
        stac_item, stac_path = _matching_population_stac_metadata(population_path)
        stac_properties = stac_item.get("properties", {})
        source_description = (
            source_tags.get("TIFFTAG_IMAGEDESCRIPTION")
            or source_tags.get("TIFFTAG_DOCUMENTNAME")
            or stac_properties.get("title")
        )
        source_label = " ".join(filter(None, (
            source_description, source_tags.get("TIFFTAG_DOCUMENTNAME"),
            Path(population_path).name,
        )))
        provider = "WorldPop" if "worldpop" in source_label.casefold() else None
        copyright_tag = source_tags.get("TIFFTAG_COPYRIGHT")
        license_id = (
            "CC-BY-4.0" if copyright_tag and "cc-by-4.0" in copyright_tag.casefold()
            else copyright_tag or stac_item.get("license")
        )
        license_url = (
            "https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by/4.0/"
            if license_id == "CC-BY-4.0" else None
        )
        doi = stac_properties.get("Digital Object Identifier (DOI)")
        if doi is None:
            doi = next((link.get("href") for link in stac_item.get("links", [])
                        if link.get("rel") == "doi"), None)

        dst_height, dst_width = map(int, shape)
        left = transform.c
        top = transform.f
        right = left + transform.a * dst_width
        bottom = top + transform.e * dst_height
        try:
            window = from_bounds(
                min(left, right), min(bottom, top), max(left, right), max(bottom, top),
                src.transform,
            ).round_offsets().round_lengths()
            window = window.intersection(Window(0, 0, src.width, src.height))
        except Exception as exc:
            raise ValueError("人口栅格与 DEM 规划范围没有重叠") from exc
        if window.width <= 0 or window.height <= 0:
            raise ValueError("人口栅格与 DEM 规划范围没有重叠")

        counts = src.read(1, window=window).astype(np.float64)
        valid = src.read_masks(1, window=window) > 0
        if src.nodata is not None:
            valid &= counts != src.nodata
        valid &= np.isfinite(counts)
        if np.any(counts[valid] < 0):
            raise ValueError("人口栅格包含负的人口数")

        source_transform = src.window_transform(window)
        geod = Geod(ellps="WGS84")
        row_areas_m2 = np.empty(counts.shape[0], dtype=np.float64)
        x0 = source_transform.c
        x1 = x0 + source_transform.a
        for row in range(counts.shape[0]):
            y0 = source_transform.f + row * source_transform.e
            y1 = y0 + source_transform.e
            area = geod.polygon_area_perimeter(
                [x0, x1, x1, x0], [y0, y0, y1, y1]
            )[0]
            row_areas_m2[row] = abs(area)
        if np.any(row_areas_m2 <= 0) or not np.isfinite(row_areas_m2).all():
            raise ValueError("无法计算人口栅格源像元面积")

        density = counts / row_areas_m2[:, None] * 1_000_000.0
        density[~valid] = np.nan
        aligned = np.full(shape, np.nan, dtype=np.float32)
        source_coverage = valid.astype(np.float32)
        aligned_coverage = np.zeros(shape, dtype=np.float32)
        reproject(
            source=source_coverage,
            destination=aligned_coverage,
            src_transform=source_transform,
            src_crs=src.crs,
            dst_transform=transform,
            dst_crs=crs,
            dst_nodata=0.0,
            resampling=Resampling.average,
            init_dest_nodata=True,
        )
        reproject(
            source=density.astype(np.float32),
            destination=aligned,
            src_transform=source_transform,
            src_crs=src.crs,
            src_nodata=np.nan,
            dst_transform=transform,
            dst_crs=crs,
            dst_nodata=np.nan,
            resampling=Resampling.average,
            init_dest_nodata=True,
        )
        aligned[aligned_coverage < 1.0 - 1e-6] = np.nan
        missing = required & (~np.isfinite(aligned) | (aligned_coverage < 1.0 - 1e-6))
        required_cells = int(required.sum())
        valid_required_cells = required_cells - int(missing.sum())
        metadata = {
            "status": ("complete" if not missing.any() else
                       "partial" if valid_required_cells else "no_coverage"),
            "source_path": str(Path(population_path).resolve()),
            "source_crs": src.crs.to_string(),
            "source_pixel_size_degrees": [abs(src.transform.a), abs(src.transform.e)],
            "source_nodata": src.nodata,
            "source_value_unit": "people_per_cell",
            "aligned_value_unit": "people_per_km2",
            "source_metadata": {
                "provider": provider,
                "asset_provider": stac_properties.get("provider"),
                "dataset_title": source_description,
                "document_name": source_tags.get("TIFFTAG_DOCUMENTNAME"),
                "copyright_tag": copyright_tag,
                "license_id": license_id,
                "license_url": license_url,
                "release": stac_properties.get("Release"),
                "version": stac_properties.get("Version"),
                "year": stac_properties.get("year"),
                "attribution": (
                    f"{provider}; dataset title: {source_description or Path(population_path).name}"
                    if provider else None
                ),
                "doi": doi,
                "metadata_file": str(stac_path.resolve()) if stac_path else None,
                "metadata_basis": ("embedded GeoTIFF tags and matching adjacent STAC item"
                                   if stac_path else
                                   "embedded GeoTIFF tags and source filename"),
            },
            "resampling": "area_weighted_average_on_density",
            "required_dem_cell_count": required_cells,
            "valid_required_cell_count": valid_required_cells,
            "missing_required_cell_count": int(missing.sum()),
            "coverage_ratio": (valid_required_cells / required_cells
                               if required_cells else 1.0),
            "nodata_policy": "preserve_as_nan_for_explicit_unknown_policy",
            "normalization_scale_people_per_km2": (
                POPULATION_DENSITY_NORMALIZATION_PEOPLE_PER_KM2),
        }
        return aligned, metadata


def population_density_cost(density_people_per_km2):
    """Convert people/km² to a fixed-scale [0, 1] soft-cost proxy."""
    density = np.asarray(density_people_per_km2, dtype=np.float32)
    valid = np.isfinite(density)
    if np.any(density[valid] < 0):
        raise ValueError("人口密度不能为负数")
    scale = POPULATION_DENSITY_NORMALIZATION_PEOPLE_PER_KM2
    cost = np.full(density.shape, np.nan, dtype=np.float32)
    cost[valid] = np.clip(
        np.log1p(density[valid]) / math.log1p(scale), 0.0, 1.0)
    return cost


def population_cost_for_routing(
        density_people_per_km2, nodata_policy="maximum", valid_mask=None):
    """Fill unknown population cells for search while retaining their mask."""
    proxy = population_density_cost(density_people_per_km2)
    unknown = ~np.isfinite(proxy)
    valid_for_imputation = ~unknown
    if valid_mask is not None:
        valid_mask = np.asarray(valid_mask, dtype=bool)
        if valid_mask.shape != proxy.shape:
            raise ValueError("population imputation mask must match the density grid")
        valid_for_imputation &= valid_mask
    if nodata_policy == "maximum":
        fill_cost = 1.0
    elif nodata_policy == "median":
        if not np.any(valid_for_imputation):
            raise ValueError("median NoData policy requires a valid population cell in the planning mask")
        fill_cost = float(np.median(proxy[valid_for_imputation]))
    elif nodata_policy == "zero":
        fill_cost = 0.0
    else:
        raise ValueError("population NoData policy must be maximum, median, or zero")
    return np.where(unknown, fill_cost, proxy).astype(np.float32), unknown


def load_dem_window(dem_path, minlon, minlat, maxlon, maxlat, max_side=WORK_MAX_SIDE):
    """读取 DEM 在给定 bbox 内的窗口，并重采样到最长边 <= max_side。
    返回 (dem, transform, crs, pixel_m)。"""
    if HAS_RASTERIO:
        with rasterio.open(dem_path) as src:
            if src.crs is None or src.crs != rasterio.crs.CRS.from_epsg(4326):
                raise ValueError("当前命令行规划仅支持带 EPSG:4326 坐标系的 DEM，请先重投影")
            crs = str(src.crs)
            # 限制到 DEM 实际范围
            dem_left, dem_bottom, dem_right, dem_top = src.bounds
            minlon = max(minlon, dem_left)
            maxlon = min(maxlon, dem_right)
            minlat = max(minlat, dem_bottom)
            maxlat = min(maxlat, dem_top)
            window = from_bounds(minlon, minlat, maxlon, maxlat, src.transform)
            window = window.round_offsets().round_lengths()
            Hn, Wn = src.height, src.width
            window = type(window)(
                col_off=max(0, window.col_off),
                row_off=max(0, window.row_off),
                width=min(window.width, Wn - window.col_off),
                height=min(window.height, Hn - window.row_off),
            )
            arr = src.read(1, window=window).astype(np.float32)
            transform = src.window_transform(window)
            nodata = src.nodata
    elif tifffile is not None:
        with tifffile.TiffFile(str(dem_path)) as tf:
            gm = tf.geotiff_metadata
            sx = gm["ModelPixelScale"][0]
            tie = gm["ModelTiepoint"]
            a = sx
            e = -sx
            c = tie[3] - tie[0] * sx
            f = tie[4] - tie[1] * (-sx)
            transform = Affine(a, 0, c, 0, e, f)
            crs = "EPSG:4326"
            full = tf.pages[0].asarray().astype(np.float32)
            nodata = -32768.0
            # bbox -> slice
            c0 = max(0, int((minlon - c) / a))
            c1 = min(full.shape[1], int((maxlon - c) / a) + 1)
            r1 = max(0, int((maxlat - f) / e))   # e<0
            r0 = min(full.shape[0], int((minlat - f) / e) + 1)
            if r0 > r1:
                r0, r1 = r1, r0
            arr = full[r0:r1, c0:c1].copy()
            transform = Affine(a, 0, c + c0 * a, 0, e, f + r0 * e)
        print("  [warn] 使用 tifffile 全量读取兜底路径")
    else:
        raise RuntimeError("需要 rasterio 或 tifffile 读取 DEM")

    # nodata 处理
    if nodata is not None:
        arr = np.where(arr == nodata, np.nan, arr)
    arr = np.where((arr < -100) | (arr > 9000), np.nan, arr)

    # 尽量保留输入 DEM 的原生网格；只有窗口超出 max_side 时才降采样。
    Hn, Wn = arr.shape
    source_lat = transform.f + transform.e * Hn / 2.0
    source_row_m, source_col_m = pixel_spacing_meters(transform, source_lat, crs)
    source_pm = math.sqrt(source_row_m * source_col_m)
    scale = max(Hn, Wn) / max_side
    if scale > 1.01:
        Hr = max(1, int(round(Hn / scale)))
        Wr = max(1, int(round(Wn / scale)))
        if HAS_SCIPY:
            from skimage.transform import resize
            arr = resize(arr, (Hr, Wr), order=1, preserve_range=True).astype(np.float32)
        left = transform.c
        top = transform.f
        right = left + transform.a * Wn
        bottom = top + transform.e * Hn
        a_new = (right - left) / Wr
        e_new = (bottom - top) / Hr
        transform = Affine(a_new, 0, left, 0, e_new, top)

    lat0 = transform.f + transform.e * (arr.shape[0] / 2.0)
    pm = pixel_meters(transform, lat0, crs)
    resample_factor = max(1.0, scale)
    print(
        f"  DEM 输入窗口 {Wn}x{Hn} / 规划网格 {arr.shape[1]}x{arr.shape[0]}  "
        f"原始像元≈{source_pm:.1f}m / 实际像元≈{pm:.1f}m  "
        f"降采样倍率={resample_factor:.2f}x  CRS={crs}"
    )
    if resample_factor > 1.25:
        print("  [warn] DEM 为适配工作网格已明显降采样；增大 --max-side 可保留更多地形细节")
    return arr, transform, crs, pm


def _fill(arr, fill=None):
    if fill is None:
        fill = np.nanmedian(arr) if np.isfinite(arr).any() else 0.0
    return np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, fill)


def _terrain_gradient(z, row_m, col_m):
    dy = np.gradient(z, row_m, axis=0) if z.shape[0] > 1 else np.zeros_like(z)
    dx = np.gradient(z, col_m, axis=1) if z.shape[1] > 1 else np.zeros_like(z)
    return dy, dx


def compute_terrain_factors(dem, pixel_m, transform=None, crs=None):
    if transform is not None:
        row_m, col_m = pixel_spacing_meters(
            transform, (transform.f + transform.e * dem.shape[0] / 2), crs)
    elif np.isscalar(pixel_m):
        row_m = col_m = float(pixel_m)
    else:
        row_m, col_m = map(float, pixel_m)
    neighborhood_m = math.sqrt(row_m * col_m)
    z = _fill(dem).astype(np.float32)
    dy, dx = _terrain_gradient(z, row_m, col_m)
    slope = np.degrees(np.arctan(np.sqrt(dx ** 2 + dy ** 2)))
    aspect = np.arctan2(dy, -dx)
    aspect_cos = np.cos(aspect)
    aspect_sin = np.sin(aspect)

    def local_std(size):
        values = z.astype(np.float64, copy=False)
        mean = ndi.uniform_filter(values, size=size, mode="reflect")
        mean_sq = ndi.uniform_filter(values * values, size=size, mode="reflect")
        np.subtract(mean_sq, mean * mean, out=mean_sq)
        np.maximum(mean_sq, 0.0, out=mean_sq)
        np.sqrt(mean_sq, out=mean_sq)
        return mean_sq.astype(np.float32)

    tri = local_std(3) if HAS_SCIPY else np.abs(z - z)

    def px_of_m(m):
        return max(1, int(round(m / neighborhood_m)))

    def tpi_at(radius_px):
        if HAS_SCIPY:
            size = 2 * radius_px + 1
            mean = ndi.uniform_filter(z, size=size, mode="reflect")
            return z - mean
        return z - z

    def rough_at(radius_px):
        if HAS_SCIPY:
            return local_std(2 * radius_px + 1)
        return np.abs(z - z)
