                (lon0 + (float(lon) - lon0 + 180.0) % 360.0 - 180.0, float(lat))
                for lon, lat in poly
            ]

        # Dissolve every reference input in source coordinates. This removes
        # repeated and partially overlapping support before projection can
        # introduce tiny non-coincident gaps or double-counted length.
        from shapely.ops import linemerge, unary_union
        reference_geometry = unary_union([
            LineString(source_coordinates(poly)) for poly in real_polys
        ])
        if reference_geometry.geom_type == "MultiLineString":
            reference_geometry = linemerge(reference_geometry)

        def line_parts(geometry):
            if geometry.geom_type == "LineString":
                if geometry.length == 0:
                    return []
                # GEOS inserts nodes at overlap boundaries. Remove only exact
                # collinear vertices so projection does not make equivalent
                # unsplit and split reference lines measure differently.
                return [geometry.simplify(0.0, preserve_topology=True)]
            if geometry.geom_type in ("MultiLineString", "GeometryCollection"):
                return [part for child in geometry.geoms for part in line_parts(child)]
            return []

        reference_lines = line_parts(reference_geometry)
        if not reference_lines:
            return None
        real_projected = [
            project_coordinates(list(line.coords)) for line in reference_lines
        ]
        planned_samples, planned_sample_weights, planned_component_lengths = _line_samples(
            [planned_projected], ROUTE_SAMPLE_STEP_M)
        real_samples, real_sample_weights, real_component_lengths = _line_samples(
            real_projected, ROUTE_SAMPLE_STEP_M)

        def segment_tree(projected_lines):
            segments = []
            for coordinates in projected_lines:
                if len(coordinates) >= 2:
                    segments.append(np.stack((coordinates[:-1], coordinates[1:]), axis=1))
            if not segments:
                raise ValueError("偏差计算线路长度必须大于零")
            return STRtree(linestrings(np.concatenate(segments, axis=0)))

        def nearest_distances(sample_coordinates, tree):
            result = np.full(len(sample_coordinates), np.inf, dtype=np.float64)
            for start in range(0, len(sample_coordinates), 50000):
                end = min(start + 50000, len(sample_coordinates))
                query_points = points(sample_coordinates[start:end])
                indices, distances = tree.query_nearest(
                    query_points, all_matches=False, return_distance=True)
                result[start + indices[0]] = distances
            if not np.isfinite(result).all():
                raise ValueError("空间索引未返回所有采样点的最近距离")
            result[result < 1e-9] = 0.0
            return result

        planned_distances = nearest_distances(planned_samples, segment_tree(real_projected))
        reference_distances = nearest_distances(real_samples, segment_tree([planned_projected]))
        planned_to_reference = _distance_summary(planned_distances, planned_sample_weights)
        reference_to_planned = _distance_summary(reference_distances, real_sample_weights)
        combined = np.concatenate((planned_distances, reference_distances))
        tolerance_f1 = {}
        for tolerance in VISUAL_TOLERANCES_M:
            precision = planned_to_reference["coverage_within_m"][str(tolerance)]
            recall = reference_to_planned["coverage_within_m"][str(tolerance)]
            tolerance_f1[str(tolerance)] = (
                2.0 * precision * recall / (precision + recall)
                if precision + recall else 0.0
            )

        reference_length_m = float(sum(real_component_lengths))
        planned_length_m = float(sum(planned_component_lengths))
        planning_window_coverage = None
        if planning_bounds_wgs84 is not None:
            left, bottom, right, top = map(float, planning_bounds_wgs84)
            window_geo = [
                (left + (right - left) * step / 20.0, bottom)
                for step in range(21)
            ] + [
                (right, bottom + (top - bottom) * step / 20.0)
                for step in range(1, 21)
            ] + [
                (right - (right - left) * step / 20.0, top)
                for step in range(1, 21)
            ] + [
                (left, top - (top - bottom) * step / 20.0)
                for step in range(1, 20)
            ]
            window_polygon = Polygon(project_coordinates(window_geo))
            reference_lines = [LineString(line) for line in real_projected if len(line) >= 2]
            inside_m = float(sum(line.intersection(window_polygon).length
                                 for line in reference_lines))
            planning_window_coverage = {
                "reference_length_within_window_m": inside_m,
                "reference_length_outside_window_m": max(reference_length_m - inside_m, 0.0),
                "reference_length_coverage_ratio": (
                    min(max(inside_m / reference_length_m, 0.0), 1.0)
                    if reference_length_m > 0.0 else None
                ),
                "bounds_wgs84": [left, bottom, right, top],
            }
        route_similarity = {
            "comparison_crs": local_crs.to_string(),
            "sampling_interval_m": ROUTE_SAMPLE_STEP_M,
            "planned_to_reference": planned_to_reference,
            "reference_to_planned": reference_to_planned,
            "symmetric": {
                "mean_m": 0.5 * (planned_to_reference["mean_m"]
                                + reference_to_planned["mean_m"]),
                "p95_m": max(planned_to_reference["p95_m"], reference_to_planned["p95_m"]),
                "sampled_hausdorff_m": float(combined.max()),
                "coverage_f1_within_m": tolerance_f1,
            },
            "length": {
                "planned_km": planned_length_m / 1000.0,
                "reference_km": reference_length_m / 1000.0,
                "signed_difference_km": (planned_length_m - reference_length_m) / 1000.0,
                "absolute_difference_km": abs(planned_length_m - reference_length_m) / 1000.0,
                "absolute_difference_pct": abs(planned_length_m - reference_length_m) / reference_length_m * 100.0,
            },
            "planning_window_coverage": planning_window_coverage,
            "endpoint_offsets": None,
            "discrete_frechet_approx": None,
            "buffer_iou": {},
        }

        reference_is_open_line = (
            len(real_projected) == 1
            and not np.allclose(real_projected[0][0], real_projected[0][-1],
                                rtol=0.0, atol=1e-6)
        )
        if reference_is_open_line:
            endpoint_a = np.asarray(planned_projected[0])
            endpoint_b = np.asarray(planned_projected[-1])
            reference_a = np.asarray(real_projected[0][0])
            reference_b = np.asarray(real_projected[0][-1])
            forward_sum = np.linalg.norm(endpoint_a - reference_a) + np.linalg.norm(endpoint_b - reference_b)
            reverse_sum = np.linalg.norm(endpoint_a - reference_b) + np.linalg.norm(endpoint_b - reference_a)
            if forward_sum <= reverse_sum:
                endpoint_offsets = (float(np.linalg.norm(endpoint_a - reference_a)),
                                    float(np.linalg.norm(endpoint_b - reference_b)))
                orientation = "forward"
            else:
                endpoint_offsets = (float(np.linalg.norm(endpoint_a - reference_b)),
                                    float(np.linalg.norm(endpoint_b - reference_a)))
                orientation = "reversed"
            route_similarity["endpoint_offsets"] = {
                "orientation": orientation,
                "planned_start_to_reference_endpoint_m": endpoint_offsets[0],
                "planned_end_to_reference_endpoint_m": endpoint_offsets[1],
            }

            planned_shape = _resample_line(planned_projected, FRECHET_SAMPLE_INTERVAL_M)
            reference_shape = _resample_line(real_projected[0], FRECHET_SAMPLE_INTERVAL_M)
            frechet = min(
                _discrete_frechet(planned_shape, reference_shape),
                _discrete_frechet(planned_shape, reference_shape[::-1]),
            )
            route_similarity["discrete_frechet_approx"] = {
                "distance_m": frechet,
                "target_max_sample_interval_m": FRECHET_SAMPLE_INTERVAL_M,
                "planned_sample_points": len(planned_shape),
                "reference_sample_points": len(reference_shape),
                "planned_max_sample_interval_m": float(
                    planned_length_m / (len(planned_shape) - 1)),
                "reference_max_sample_interval_m": float(
                    reference_length_m / (len(reference_shape) - 1)),
            }

        try:
            from shapely.ops import unary_union
            planned_line = LineString(planned_projected)
            reference_geometry = unary_union([LineString(line) for line in real_projected])
            for radius in (100, 1000):
                planned_buffer = planned_line.buffer(radius, quad_segs=8)
                reference_buffer = reference_geometry.buffer(radius, quad_segs=8)
                union_area = planned_buffer.union(reference_buffer).area
                route_similarity["buffer_iou"][str(radius)] = (
                    float(planned_buffer.intersection(reference_buffer).area / union_area)
                    if union_area else 1.0
                )
        except Exception:
            route_similarity["buffer_iou"] = {}

        return {
            "route_similarity_version": 6,
            "route_similarity": route_similarity,
        }
    except Exception as ex:
        print(f"    [warn] 偏差计算失败: {ex}")
        return None


def eval_error(plan_len_km, dev):
    """Return v4 arc-length-weighted geometry and length metrics."""
    if not dev:
        return None
    similarity = dev.get("route_similarity")
    if not similarity:
        return None
    plan_len = float(plan_len_km or 0.0)
    reference_len = float(similarity["length"]["reference_km"])
    if reference_len <= 0:
        return None
    length = dict(similarity["length"])
    length.update({
        "planned_km": plan_len,
        "signed_difference_km": plan_len - reference_len,
        "absolute_difference_km": abs(plan_len - reference_len),
        "absolute_difference_pct": abs(plan_len - reference_len) / reference_len * 100.0,
    })
    similarity = dict(similarity)
    similarity["length"] = length
    return {
        "route_similarity_version": 6,
        "route_similarity": similarity,
    }


def eval_planned_route_error(planned_geo, dev):
    """Score using full route length; output metrics are rounded for display only."""
    return eval_error(path_length_km(planned_geo), dev)


def load_real_cases_csv(path):
    """从 real_cases.csv 载入测试用例列表 (name,(lat,lon),(lat,lon),None)。
    CSV 列: name,start_lat,start_lon,end_lat,end_lon。SHP(<净化名>_real_path.shp)
    与 CSV 同目录, 由 resolve_real_path 自动匹配。"""
    import csv as _csv
    out = []
    p = Path(path)
    if not p.is_file():
        raise ValueError(f"--real-cases-csv 文件不存在: {path}")
    with open(p, encoding="utf-8-sig", newline="") as f:
        reader = _csv.DictReader(f)
        fieldnames = reader.fieldnames or []
        if any(not field for field in fieldnames) or len(fieldnames) != len(set(fieldnames)):
            raise ValueError("--real-cases-csv 表头存在空列名或重复列名")
        required = {"name", "start_lat", "start_lon", "end_lat", "end_lon"}
        missing = required - set(fieldnames)
        if missing:
            raise ValueError(f"--real-cases-csv 缺少列: {', '.join(sorted(missing))}")
        for row_number, row in enumerate(reader, start=2):
            if None in row:
                raise ValueError(f"--real-cases-csv 第 {row_number} 行字段数多于表头")
            try:
                name = str(row.get("name") or "").strip()
                s = (float(row["start_lat"]), float(row["start_lon"]))
                e = (float(row["end_lat"]), float(row["end_lon"]))
            except (KeyError, TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
                raise ValueError(f"--real-cases-csv 第 {row_number} 行任务字段无效: {exc}") from exc
            if not name:
                raise ValueError(f"--real-cases-csv 第 {row_number} 行缺少任务名称")
            if not all(math.isfinite(value) for value in (*s, *e)):
                raise ValueError(f"--real-cases-csv 第 {row_number} 行坐标必须为有限数值")
            if not (-90 <= s[0] <= 90 and -90 <= e[0] <= 90
                    and -180 <= s[1] <= 180 and -180 <= e[1] <= 180):
                raise ValueError(f"--real-cases-csv 第 {row_number} 行经纬度超出有效范围")
            out.append((name, s, e, None))
    print(f"[真实对比] 从 CSV 载入 {len(out)} 条真实走廊用例: {p}")
    return out


def select_original_line(shp_path, transform, H, W, dem_crs):
    """自动选用与 DEM 范围重叠的现有线路作为原始对比线。
    本数据集(台湾 DEM vs 中国 SHP)地理不重叠时返回 None（由主流程降级处理）。"""
    if not (HAS_GPD and shp_path and Path(shp_path).exists()):
        return None, None, None, None
    left = transform.c
    bottom = transform.f + transform.e * H
    right = transform.c + transform.a * W
    top = transform.f
    dem_box = box(left, bottom, right, top)
    try:
        gdf = gpd.read_file(shp_path)
        if dem_crs and gdf.crs and str(gdf.crs) != str(dem_crs):
            gdf = gdf.to_crs(dem_crs)
        elif dem_crs and gdf.crs is None:
            gdf = gdf.set_crs(dem_crs, allow_override=True)
    except Exception as ex:
        print(f"    [warn] 读取 SHP 失败: {ex}")
        return None, None, None, None

    def endpoint_ok(lat, lon):
        if not (left - 1e-6 <= lon <= right + 1e-6 and bottom - 1e-6 <= lat <= top + 1e-6):
            return False
        r, c = geo_to_rowcol(lat, lon, transform)
        r = min(max(r, 0), H - 1); c = min(max(c, 0), W - 1)
        return True

    candidates = []
    for geom in gdf.geometry:
        if geom is None or geom.is_empty:
            continue
        try:
            if not geom.intersects(dem_box):
                continue
        except Exception:
            continue
        sub = geom if geom.geom_type != "MultiLineString" else max(geom.geoms, key=lambda g: g.length)
        coords = list(sub.coords)
        if len(coords) < 2:
            continue
        s, e = coords[0], coords[-1]
        if not (endpoint_ok(s[1], s[0]) and endpoint_ok(e[1], e[0])):
            continue
        candidates.append((sub, sub.length))
    if not candidates:
        print("    [info] 未找到与 DEM 重叠的现有线路（台湾 DEM 与中国 SHP 地理不重叠）")
        return None, None, None, None
    sub, _ = max(candidates, key=lambda t: t[1])
    coords = list(sub.coords)
    orig_ll = [(x, y) for x, y in coords]
    return orig_ll, (coords[0][1], coords[0][0]), (coords[-1][1], coords[-1][0]), Path(shp_path).name
# ===== transmission_path_planner/official_hazards.py =====
"""Read official ARDSWC MVT hazard tiles for a WGS84 planning window.

The returned masks are screening inputs only. In particular, mapped potential
landslide and debris-flow impact areas are never interpreted as legal exclusions.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import math
import re
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np


LAYER_SPECS = {
    "debris_flow_impact": "DEBRIS1753_20260126",
    "landslide_potential": "POTENTIAL_LANDSLIDE_20260106",
    "landslide_impact": "POTENTIAL_LANDSLIDE_AFFECT_20260106",
}


class _DriverUnavailable(RuntimeError):
    pass


class _DependencyUnavailable(RuntimeError):
    pass


def _zero_masks(shape):
    return {name: np.zeros(shape, dtype=np.uint8) for name in LAYER_SPECS}


def _tile_xy(lon, lat, zoom):
    n = 1 << zoom
    x = math.floor((lon + 180.0) / 360.0 * n)
    lat = max(min(lat, 85.0511287798066), -85.0511287798066)
    lat_rad = math.radians(lat)
    y = math.floor((1.0 - math.asinh(math.tan(lat_rad)) / math.pi) * 0.5 * n)
    return min(max(x, 0), n - 1), min(max(y, 0), n - 1)


def _tiles_for_bbox(bbox, zoom):
    west, south, east, north = bbox
    if not all(math.isfinite(float(value)) for value in bbox):
        raise ValueError("bbox_wgs84 坐标必须是有限数值")
    if not (-180 <= west < east <= 180 and -90 <= south < north <= 90):
        raise ValueError("bbox_wgs84 必须是 west,south,east,north 顺序的有效范围")

    # Work in fractional tile coordinates before applying nextafter: at a
    # geographic tile edge, the ULP in degrees can vanish in the Mercator math.
    n = 1 << zoom
    x_west = (west + 180.0) / 360.0 * n
    x_east = (east + 180.0) / 360.0 * n

    def y_fraction(lat):
        lat = max(min(lat, 85.0511287798066), -85.0511287798066)
        lat_rad = math.radians(lat)
        return (1.0 - math.asinh(math.tan(lat_rad)) / math.pi) * 0.5 * n

    y_north = y_fraction(north)
    y_south = y_fraction(south)
    x0 = math.floor(math.nextafter(x_west, math.inf))
    x1 = math.floor(math.nextafter(x_east, -math.inf))
    y0 = math.floor(math.nextafter(y_north, math.inf))
    y1 = math.floor(math.nextafter(y_south, -math.inf))
    x0, x1 = min(max(x0, 0), n - 1), min(max(x1, 0), n - 1)
    y0, y1 = min(max(y0, 0), n - 1), min(max(y1, 0), n - 1)
    return [(x, y) for y in range(min(y0, y1), max(y0, y1) + 1)
            for x in range(min(x0, x1), max(x0, x1) + 1)]


def _bbox_is_covered(requested, source):
    west, south, east, north = requested
    src_west, src_south, src_east, src_north = source
    return (src_west <= west and src_south <= south
            and src_east >= east and src_north >= north)


def _failed_tile_info(raw_failures, layer_dir, zoom):
    """Return identifiable failed (x,y) tiles and count of unlocated failures."""
    failed = set()
    unlocated = 0
    for item in raw_failures or []:
        values = item if isinstance(item, dict) else {}
        if values:
            try:
                if int(values.get("z", zoom)) == zoom:
                    failed.add((int(values["x"]), int(values["y"])))
                    continue
            except (KeyError, TypeError, ValueError):
                pass
        match = re.search(r"(?:^|[/\\])\d+[/\\](\d+)[/\\](\d+)\.pbf(?:$|[?#])", str(item))
        if match:
            y, x = map(int, match.groups())
            failed.add((x, y))
        else:
            unlocated += 1
    return failed, unlocated


def _load_checksums(path):
    if not path.is_file():
        return None
    checksums = {}
    for line in path.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines():
        match = re.match(r"^([0-9a-fA-F]{64})\s+\*?(.+?)\s*$", line)
        if match:
            checksums[match.group(2).replace("\\", "/")] = match.group(1).lower()
    return checksums


def _sha256(path):
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with path.open("rb") as source:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: source.read(1024 * 1024), b""):
            digest.update(chunk)
    return digest.hexdigest()


def _read_tile_geometries(tile_path, bbox_wgs84, tile_x, tile_y, zoom):
    """Read polygon fragments through GDAL's MVT driver and return WGS84 shapes."""
    try:
        import pyogrio
    except ImportError as exc:
        raise _DriverUnavailable("pyogrio is not installed") from exc

    # The downloaded BigGIS package stores paths as z/y/x, while GDAL's MVT
    # driver assumes z/x/y from a path unless explicit tile indices are given.
    tile_options = {"X": int(tile_x), "Y": int(tile_y), "Z": int(zoom)}
    info = pyogrio.read_info(tile_path, layer=0, **tile_options)
    if info.get("driver") != "MVT":
        raise _DriverUnavailable(f"GDAL opened tile with {info.get('driver')!r}, not MVT")
    source_crs = info.get("crs")
    if not source_crs:
        raise ValueError("MVT tile does not declare a CRS")

    try:
        from pyproj import CRS, Transformer
        from shapely.geometry import box
        from shapely.ops import transform as transform_geometry
        from shapely import from_wkb
    except ImportError as exc:
        raise _DependencyUnavailable("pyproj and shapely are required to reproject MVT polygons") from exc

    source = CRS.from_user_input(source_crs)
    target = CRS.from_epsg(4326)
    forward = Transformer.from_crs(target, source, always_xy=True)
    backward = Transformer.from_crs(source, target, always_xy=True)
    source_bounds = forward.transform_bounds(*bbox_wgs84, densify_pts=21)
    layer_name = info.get("layer_name") or 0
    _, _, geometries, _ = pyogrio.raw.read(
        tile_path, layer=layer_name, columns=[], bbox=source_bounds, **tile_options,
    )
    if geometries is None:
        return []

    window = box(*bbox_wgs84)
    result = []
    for value in geometries:
        if value is None:
            continue
        geometry = from_wkb(bytes(value))
        if geometry.is_empty:
            continue
        if geometry.geom_type not in ("Polygon", "MultiPolygon"):
            continue
        if not source.equals(target):
            geometry = transform_geometry(backward.transform, geometry)
        if geometry.is_empty or not geometry.intersects(window):
            continue
        clipped = geometry.intersection(window)
        if clipped.geom_type in ("Polygon", "MultiPolygon") and not clipped.is_empty:
            result.append(clipped)
        elif clipped.geom_type == "GeometryCollection":
            result.extend(g for g in clipped.geoms
                          if g.geom_type in ("Polygon", "MultiPolygon") and not g.is_empty)
    return result


def _rasterize_union(geometries, transform, shape):
    if not geometries:
        return np.zeros(shape, dtype=np.uint8)
    try:
        from rasterio.features import rasterize
    except ImportError as exc:
        raise _DependencyUnavailable("rasterio is required to rasterize official hazard tiles") from exc
    return rasterize(
        ((geometry, 1) for geometry in geometries),
        out_shape=shape,
        transform=transform,
        fill=0,
        all_touched=True,
        dtype="uint8",
    )


def load_official_hazard_masks(source_dir, bbox_wgs84, transform, shape):
    """Load three official hazard masks for a WGS84 DEM window.

    Parameters
    ----------
    source_dir:
        Directory containing the downloaded ARDSWC MVT package and manifest.
    bbox_wgs84:
        ``(west, south, east, north)`` in longitude/latitude degrees.
    transform, shape:
        Affine transform and ``(height, width)`` of the DEM window. The grid is
        assumed to use EPSG:4326, matching the planner's current DEM contract.

    Returns
    -------
    (masks, metadata):
        uint8 0/1 masks keyed by ``debris_flow_impact``,
        ``landslide_potential``, and ``landslide_impact``. Duplicate MVT tile
        fragments are combined by raster union. Potential and impact masks are
        screening inputs only and never imply a hard exclusion.
    """
    bbox = tuple(float(v) for v in bbox_wgs84)
    if len(bbox) != 4:
        raise ValueError("bbox_wgs84 必须包含 west,south,east,north 四个坐标")
    if len(shape) != 2 or any(int(v) <= 0 for v in shape):
        raise ValueError("shape 必须是正值 (height, width)")
    masks = _zero_masks(tuple(map(int, shape)))
    root = Path(source_dir) if source_dir is not None else None
    metadata = {
        "status": "missing_source",
        "coverage_status": "unknown",
        "source_dir": str(root.resolve()) if root is not None and root.exists() else str(root) if root else None,
        "source": None,
        "downloaded_at_utc": None,
        "source_bbox_wgs84": None,
        "requested_bbox_wgs84": list(bbox),
        "zoom": None,
        "raster_crs": "EPSG:4326",
        "risk_mode": "soft_only_screening; no hard exclusion",
        "warning": "MVT tiles are scale-generalized and clipped at tile boundaries; not original survey polygons.",
        "tiles": {"requested": 0, "read": 0, "official_empty": 0,
                  "failed": 0, "unknown": 0, "read_error": 0,
                  "checksum_mismatch": 0},
        "layers": {},
    }
    for key, layer_dir in LAYER_SPECS.items():
        metadata["layers"][key] = {
            "source_layer": layer_dir,
            "planning_role": "soft_risk_only",
            "default_soft_weight": 0.0,
            "hard_exclusion_allowed": False,
            "status": "not_loaded",
            "coverage_status": "unknown",
            "feature_fragment_count": 0,
            "non_polygon_feature_count": 0,
            "covered_cell_count": 0,
            "tile_records": [],
            "tiles": {"requested": 0, "read": 0, "official_empty": 0,
                      "failed": 0, "unknown": 0, "read_error": 0,
                      "checksum_mismatch": 0},
        }

    if root is None or not root.is_dir():
        metadata["warning"] = "Official hazard source directory is missing; all masks are zero and coverage is unknown."
        return masks, metadata

    manifest_path = root / "manifest.json"
    if not manifest_path.is_file():
        metadata.update(status="no_manifest", coverage_status="unknown")
        metadata["warning"] = "Manifest is missing; absent tiles cannot be distinguished from official empty tiles."
        return masks, metadata
    try:
        manifest = json.loads(manifest_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        zoom = int(manifest["zoom"])
        source_bbox = (float(manifest["bbox_wgs84"]["west"]),
                       float(manifest["bbox_wgs84"]["south"]),
                       float(manifest["bbox_wgs84"]["east"]),
                       float(manifest["bbox_wgs84"]["north"]))
        tile_x_range = tuple(map(int, manifest["tile_x_range_inclusive"]))
        tile_y_range = tuple(map(int, manifest["tile_y_range_inclusive"]))
        layer_manifest = manifest["layers"]
        if zoom < 0 or len(source_bbox) != 4 or len(tile_x_range) != 2 or len(tile_y_range) != 2:
            raise ValueError("invalid manifest fields")
    except (KeyError, TypeError, ValueError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
        metadata.update(status="invalid_manifest", coverage_status="unknown")
        metadata["warning"] = f"Official hazard manifest is invalid: {exc}"
        return masks, metadata

    metadata.update(
        status="available",
        source=manifest.get("source"),
        downloaded_at_utc=manifest.get("downloaded_at_utc"),
        source_bbox_wgs84=list(source_bbox),
        zoom=zoom,
        manifest_path=str(manifest_path.resolve()),
    )
    requested_tiles = _tiles_for_bbox(bbox, zoom)
    metadata["tiles"]["requested"] = len(requested_tiles) * len(LAYER_SPECS)
    source_bbox_covers = _bbox_is_covered(bbox, source_bbox)
    checksums = _load_checksums(root / "tiles.sha256")
    all_layers_complete = True
    saw_data = False
    saw_read_error = False
    saw_driver_unavailable = False
    saw_dependency_unavailable = False

    for key, layer_dir in LAYER_SPECS.items():
        layer_meta = layer_manifest.get(layer_dir)
        layer_out = metadata["layers"][key]
        if not isinstance(layer_meta, dict):
            layer_out.update(status="missing_manifest_layer", coverage_status="unknown")
            layer_out["tiles"]["unknown"] = len(requested_tiles)
            metadata["tiles"]["unknown"] += len(requested_tiles)
            all_layers_complete = False
            continue

        layer_out.update(
            description=layer_meta.get("description"),
            source_date=(re.search(r"_(\d{8})$", layer_dir).group(1)
                         if re.search(r"_(\d{8})$", layer_dir) else None),
        )
        counts_valid = False
        checksum_count_valid = None
        checksummed_nonempty_count = None
        try:
            requested_count = int(layer_meta["tiles_requested"])
            nonempty_count = int(layer_meta["nonempty_tiles"])
            empty_count = int(layer_meta["empty_tiles"])
            raw_failures = layer_meta.get("failures", layer_meta.get("failed_tiles", [])) or []
            failed_declared = int(layer_meta.get("failed_tile_count", len(raw_failures)))
            failed_coords, unlocated_failures = _failed_tile_info(raw_failures, layer_dir, zoom)
            expected_rectangle_count = ((tile_x_range[1] - tile_x_range[0] + 1)
                                        * (tile_y_range[1] - tile_y_range[0] + 1))
            counts_valid = (requested_count == expected_rectangle_count
                            and nonempty_count + empty_count + failed_declared == requested_count)
            if checksums is not None:
                layer_checksum_prefix = f"{layer_dir}/"
                layer_checksum_paths = [
                    path for path in checksums if path.startswith(layer_checksum_prefix)
                ]
                checksummed_coords = set()
                checksum_paths_valid = True
                for path in layer_checksum_paths:
                    match = re.fullmatch(
                        rf"{re.escape(layer_dir)}/{zoom}/(\d+)/(\d+)\.pbf", path)
                    if not match:
                        checksum_paths_valid = False
                        continue
                    tile_y, tile_x = map(int, match.groups())
                    if not (tile_x_range[0] <= tile_x <= tile_x_range[1]
                            and tile_y_range[0] <= tile_y <= tile_y_range[1]):
                        checksum_paths_valid = False
                        continue
                    checksummed_coords.add((tile_x, tile_y))
                checksummed_nonempty_count = len(checksummed_coords)
                checksum_count_valid = (
                    checksum_paths_valid
                    and len(layer_checksum_paths) == checksummed_nonempty_count
                    and checksummed_nonempty_count == nonempty_count
                )
        except (KeyError, TypeError, ValueError):
            requested_count = nonempty_count = empty_count = failed_declared = 0
            failed_coords, unlocated_failures = set(), 0
            checksum_count_valid = False if checksums is not None else None
            checksummed_nonempty_count = 0 if checksums is not None else None

        layer_complete = (source_bbox_covers and counts_valid
                          and checksum_count_valid is not False)
        layer_geometries = []
        tile_counts = layer_out["tiles"]
        tile_counts["requested"] = len(requested_tiles)
        metadata["layers"][key]["manifest_counts_valid"] = counts_valid
        metadata["layers"][key]["tiles"]["manifest_nonempty"] = nonempty_count
        metadata["layers"][key]["tiles"]["manifest_official_empty"] = empty_count
        metadata["layers"][key]["tiles"]["manifest_failed"] = failed_declared
        metadata["layers"][key]["tiles"]["checksummed_nonempty"] = checksummed_nonempty_count
        metadata["layers"][key]["manifest_checksum_count_match"] = checksum_count_valid
        layer_root = root / layer_dir

        def record_tile(x, y, status, **details):
            layer_out["tile_records"].append({
                "z": zoom, "x": x, "y": y,
                "path": f"{layer_dir}/{zoom}/{y}/{x}.pbf",
                "status": status,
                **details,
            })

        for x, y in requested_tiles:
            tile_key = (x, y)
            relpath = f"{layer_dir}/{zoom}/{y}/{x}.pbf"
            tile_path = layer_root / str(zoom) / str(y) / f"{x}.pbf"
            if (x < tile_x_range[0] or x > tile_x_range[1]
                    or y < tile_y_range[0] or y > tile_y_range[1]):
                tile_counts["unknown"] += 1
                record_tile(x, y, "unknown_outside_declared_coverage")
                layer_complete = False
                continue
            if tile_key in failed_coords:
                tile_counts["failed"] += 1
                record_tile(x, y, "download_failed")
                layer_complete = False
                continue
            if not tile_path.is_file():
                if (source_bbox_covers and counts_valid and checksum_count_valid is True
                        and relpath not in checksums
                        and unlocated_failures == 0):
                    tile_counts["official_empty"] += 1
                    record_tile(x, y, "official_empty")
                else:
                    tile_counts["unknown"] += 1
                    record_tile(x, y, "missing_unknown")
                    layer_complete = False
                continue

            checksum_status = "not_available"
            if checksums is not None:
                expected_hash = checksums.get(relpath)
                if expected_hash and _sha256(tile_path) != expected_hash:
                    tile_counts["checksum_mismatch"] += 1
                    record_tile(x, y, "checksum_mismatch")
                    layer_complete = False
                    continue
                if expected_hash is None:
                    checksum_status = "not_listed"
                    layer_complete = False
                else:
                    checksum_status = "verified"
            try:
                tile_geometries = _read_tile_geometries(tile_path, bbox, x, y, zoom)
            except _DriverUnavailable as exc:
                saw_driver_unavailable = True
                layer_out["warning"] = str(exc)
                tile_counts["read_error"] += 1
                record_tile(x, y, "driver_unavailable", checksum_status=checksum_status,
                            error=str(exc))
                layer_complete = False
                continue
            except _DependencyUnavailable as exc:
                saw_dependency_unavailable = True
                layer_out["warning"] = str(exc)
                tile_counts["read_error"] += 1
                record_tile(x, y, "dependency_unavailable", checksum_status=checksum_status,
                            error=str(exc))
                layer_complete = False
                continue
            except Exception as exc:
                saw_read_error = True
                layer_out.setdefault("warnings", []).append(
                    f"{relpath}: {type(exc).__name__}: {exc}")
                tile_counts["read_error"] += 1
                record_tile(x, y, "read_error", checksum_status=checksum_status,
                            error=f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}")
                layer_complete = False
                continue
            tile_counts["read"] += 1
            metadata["tiles"]["read"] += 1
            layer_geometries.extend(tile_geometries)
            record_tile(x, y, "read", checksum_status=checksum_status,
                        feature_fragment_count=len(tile_geometries))

        layer_out["feature_fragment_count"] = len(layer_geometries)
        if layer_geometries:
            saw_data = True
            try:
                layer_mask = _rasterize_union(layer_geometries, transform, tuple(map(int, shape)))
            except _DriverUnavailable as exc:
                saw_driver_unavailable = True
                layer_out["warning"] = str(exc)
                layer_mask = np.zeros(shape, dtype=np.uint8)
                layer_complete = False
            except _DependencyUnavailable as exc:
                saw_dependency_unavailable = True
                layer_out["warning"] = str(exc)
                layer_mask = np.zeros(shape, dtype=np.uint8)
                layer_complete = False
            except Exception as exc:
                saw_read_error = True
                layer_out["warning"] = f"rasterization failed: {type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"
                layer_mask = np.zeros(shape, dtype=np.uint8)
                layer_complete = False
            masks[key] = (np.asarray(layer_mask) > 0).astype(np.uint8)
            layer_out["covered_cell_count"] = int(np.count_nonzero(masks[key]))

        for count_name in ("official_empty", "failed", "unknown", "read_error", "checksum_mismatch"):
            metadata["tiles"][count_name] += tile_counts[count_name]
        layer_out["coverage_status"] = "complete" if layer_complete else "partial"
        if layer_complete:
            if layer_geometries:
                layer_out["status"] = "available"
            elif tile_counts["official_empty"] == len(requested_tiles):
                layer_out["status"] = "official_empty"
            else:
                layer_out["status"] = "empty_in_window"
        else:
            layer_out["status"] = "partial"
            all_layers_complete = False

    metadata["coverage_status"] = "complete" if all_layers_complete else "partial"
    if saw_driver_unavailable:
        metadata["status"] = "driver_unavailable"
    elif saw_dependency_unavailable:
        metadata["status"] = "dependency_unavailable"
    elif saw_read_error:
        metadata["status"] = "partial"
    elif saw_data:
        metadata["status"] = "available" if all_layers_complete else "partial"
    elif all_layers_complete:
        metadata["status"] = "official_empty"
    else:
        metadata["status"] = "partial"
    return masks, metadata
# ===== transmission_path_planner/osm.py =====
"""OpenStreetMap 矢量数据的栅格化与距离/密度/用地波段。"""

import ast
from shapely import make_valid
from shapely.ops import unary_union

from .config import *
from .geometry import *
from .data import _fill, _terrain_gradient, _landuse_tag_to_code

_PKG_CACHE = {}
_PKL_CACHE = _PKG_CACHE

class OSMBands(dict):
    """栅格波段字典；状态元数据放在属性中，不影响 ``items()`` 的数组接口。"""

    def __init__(self, *args, metadata=None, geometries=None, **kwargs):
        super().__init__(*args, **kwargs)
        self.metadata = metadata or {}
        self.geometries = geometries or {}


def load_osm_geoms_with_status(pkl_path, geom_type, bbox):
    """读取 Overpass 缓存并返回 (bbox 内几何, 图层状态元数据)。

    ``uncovered_bbox`` 表示请求框完全位于缓存中观察到的要素范围之外；
    ``empty_in_bbox`` 表示范围框相交但没有要素命中。跳过范围外非有限坐标要素时，
    ``coverage_status`` 标记为 ``partial``，因为无法确认缺失坐标是否影响请求范围。
    OSM 覆盖完整性仍需另行核验。
    """
    pkl_path = Path(pkl_path)
    metadata = {
        "path": str(pkl_path), "status": None, "source_element_count": 0,
        "geometry_count_in_bbox": 0, "observed_feature_bounds": None,
        "coverage_basis": "observed_feature_extent",
        "tag_wrapper_count": 0, "tag_parse_error_count": 0,
        "geometry_repaired_count": 0,
        "nonfinite_geometry_outside_bbox_count": 0,
    }
    if not HAS_SHAPELY:
        metadata.update(status="unavailable_dependency", reason="shapely_missing")
        return [], metadata
    if not pkl_path.exists():
        metadata.update(status="missing_file", reason="cache_file_not_found")
        return [], metadata
    if pkl_path in _PKL_CACHE:
        data = _PKL_CACHE[pkl_path]
    else:
        try:
            with open(pkl_path, "rb") as f:
                data = pickle.load(f)
            _PKL_CACHE[pkl_path] = data
        except Exception as exc:
            metadata.update(status="read_error", reason=type(exc).__name__)
            return [], metadata
    if not isinstance(data, dict):
        metadata.update(status="invalid_cache", reason="expected_overpass_object")
        return [], metadata
    elems = data.get("elements", [])
    if not isinstance(elems, list):
        metadata.update(status="invalid_cache", reason="elements_not_a_list")
        return [], metadata
    metadata["source_element_count"] = len(elems)
    if not elems:
        metadata.update(status="empty_source", coverage_status="unknown")
        return [], metadata
    box_poly = box(bbox[0], bbox[1], bbox[2], bbox[3])
    out = []
    extent = [float("inf"), float("inf"), float("-inf"), float("-inf")]
    usable_geometry_count = 0
    for el in elems:
        if not isinstance(el, dict):
            continue
        etype = el.get("type")
        if etype not in ("way", "relation"):
            continue
        raw_tags = el.get("tags", {}) or {}
        tags = raw_tags
        if isinstance(raw_tags, dict) and set(raw_tags) == {"tags"}:
            raw_tags = raw_tags["tags"]
            metadata["tag_wrapper_count"] += 1
        if isinstance(raw_tags, str):
            try:
                raw_tags = ast.literal_eval(raw_tags)
            except (SyntaxError, ValueError):
                raw_tags = None
            if not isinstance(raw_tags, dict):
                metadata["tag_parse_error_count"] += 1
                tags = {}
            else:
                tags = raw_tags
        elif isinstance(raw_tags, dict):
            tags = raw_tags
        elif raw_tags:
            metadata["tag_parse_error_count"] += 1
            tags = {}
        # 收集该元素的所有几何坐标列表:
        #  - way: 直接用 top-level geometry;
        #  - relation(multipolygon, OSM landuse 多为 relation): 优先 geometry,
        #    否则遍历 members 中 type=="way" 的成员几何(member 通常无 geometry 顶层字段)。
        geom_lists = []
        if etype == "way":
            g = el.get("geometry")
            if g:
                geom_lists.append(g)
        else:  # relation
            g = el.get("geometry")
            if g:
                geom_lists.append(g)
            else:
                for m in el.get("members", []) or []:
                    if m.get("type") == "way":
                        mg = m.get("geometry")
                        if mg:
                            geom_lists.append(mg)
        for g in geom_lists:
            if not g:
                continue
            try:
                coords = [(float(pt["lon"]), float(pt["lat"])) for pt in g]
            except (KeyError, TypeError, ValueError):
                continue
            finite_coords = [point for point in coords
                             if math.isfinite(point[0]) and math.isfinite(point[1])]
            has_nonfinite = len(finite_coords) != len(coords)
            if has_nonfinite and not finite_coords:
                raise ValueError(
                    f"OSM 要素 {el.get('id', 'unknown')} 含无法定位的非有限坐标，拒绝规划")
            bounds_coords = finite_coords if has_nonfinite else coords
            if bounds_coords:
                min_x = min(point[0] for point in bounds_coords)
                min_y = min(point[1] for point in bounds_coords)
                max_x = max(point[0] for point in bounds_coords)
                max_y = max(point[1] for point in bounds_coords)
                extent[0] = min(extent[0], min_x)
                extent[1] = min(extent[1], min_y)
                extent[2] = max(extent[2], max_x)
                extent[3] = max(extent[3], max_y)

            if has_nonfinite:
                if (max_x < bbox[0] or min_x > bbox[2]
                        or max_y < bbox[1] or min_y > bbox[3]):
                    metadata["nonfinite_geometry_outside_bbox_count"] += 1
                    continue
                raise ValueError(
                    f"OSM 要素 {el.get('id', 'unknown')} 在请求范围内含非有限坐标，拒绝规划")

            min_coords = 1 if geom_type == "point" else 3 if geom_type == "polygon" else 2
            if len(coords) < min_coords:
                continue
            usable_geometry_count += 1
            if (max_x < bbox[0] or min_x > bbox[2]
                    or max_y < bbox[1] or min_y > bbox[3]):
                continue
            try:
                if geom_type == "point":
                    geom = Point(coords[0])
                elif geom_type == "polygon":
                    if coords[0] != coords[-1]:
                        coords = coords + [coords[0]]
                    geom = Polygon(coords)
                else:
                    geom = LineString(coords)
            except Exception:
                usable_geometry_count -= 1
                continue
            if geom.is_empty:
                usable_geometry_count -= 1
                continue
            if geom_type == "polygon" and not geom.is_valid:
                try:
                    geom = make_valid(geom)
                    if geom.geom_type == "GeometryCollection":
                        polygons = []
                        for part in geom.geoms:
                            if part.geom_type == "Polygon":
                                polygons.append(part)
                            elif part.geom_type == "MultiPolygon":
                                polygons.extend(part.geoms)
                        geom = unary_union(polygons) if polygons else geom
                except Exception as exc:
                    raise ValueError(
                        f"OSM 面要素 {el.get('id', 'unknown')} 几何修复失败") from exc
                if (geom.is_empty or not geom.is_valid
                        or geom.geom_type not in {"Polygon", "MultiPolygon"}):
                    raise ValueError(
                        f"OSM 面要素 {el.get('id', 'unknown')} 几何修复后仍不可用")
                metadata["geometry_repaired_count"] += 1
            if box_poly.intersects(geom):
                out.append((geom, tags))

    if extent[0] != float("inf"):
        metadata["observed_feature_bounds"] = tuple(extent)
    metadata["geometry_count_in_bbox"] = len(out)
    if out:
        metadata.update(status="available", coverage_status="features_observed")
    elif usable_geometry_count == 0:
        metadata.update(status="no_usable_geometry", coverage_status="unknown")
    elif (extent[2] < bbox[0] or extent[0] > bbox[2]
          or extent[3] < bbox[1] or extent[1] > bbox[3]):
        metadata.update(status="uncovered_bbox", coverage_status="outside_observed_feature_extent")
    else:
        metadata.update(status="empty_in_bbox", coverage_status="unverified_within_feature_extent")
    if metadata["nonfinite_geometry_outside_bbox_count"]:
        metadata.update(
            coverage_status="partial",
            coverage_reason="nonfinite_geometry_skipped_outside_bbox",
        )
    return out, metadata


def load_osm_geoms(pkl_path, geom_type, bbox):
    """兼容接口：仅返回 bbox 内几何列表。"""
    geoms, _ = load_osm_geoms_with_status(pkl_path, geom_type, bbox)
    return geoms


def rasterize_on(geoms, transform, shape):
    if not geoms:
        return np.zeros(shape, np.uint8)
    shapes = [(g, 1) for g, _ in geoms]
    return rio_rasterize(shapes, out_shape=shape, transform=transform,
                         fill=0, all_touched=True, dtype=np.uint8)


def _pixel_spacing_for_shape(transform, shape, crs=None):
    lat_center = transform.f + transform.b * (shape[1] / 2.0) + transform.e * (shape[0] / 2.0)
    return pixel_spacing_meters(transform, lat_center, crs)


def compute_distance_band(geoms, transform, shape, max_dist_m=5000.0, *, crs=None):
    row_m, col_m = _pixel_spacing_for_shape(transform, shape, crs)
    pad_rows = int(math.ceil(max_dist_m / row_m)) + 1
    pad_cols = int(math.ceil(max_dist_m / col_m)) + 1
    padded_shape = (shape[0] + 2 * pad_rows, shape[1] + 2 * pad_cols)
    padded_transform = transform * Affine.translation(-pad_cols, -pad_rows)
    on = rasterize_on(geoms, padded_transform, padded_shape)
    if on.sum() == 0:
        return np.full(shape, max_dist_m, np.float32)
    distance_m = distance_transform_edt(1 - on, sampling=(row_m, col_m))
    cropped = distance_m[pad_rows:pad_rows + shape[0],
                         pad_cols:pad_cols + shape[1]]
    return np.clip(cropped, 0, max_dist_m).astype(np.float32)


def compute_building_density(geoms, transform, shape, *, crs=None):
    """Return a 500 m-smoothed share of cells touched by mapped building footprints."""
    row_m, col_m = _pixel_spacing_for_shape(transform, shape, crs)
    sigma = (500.0 / row_m, 500.0 / col_m)
    pad_rows = int(math.ceil(4.0 * sigma[0]))
    pad_cols = int(math.ceil(4.0 * sigma[1]))
    padded_shape = (shape[0] + 2 * pad_rows, shape[1] + 2 * pad_cols)
    padded_transform = transform * Affine.translation(-pad_cols, -pad_rows)
    on = rasterize_on(geoms, padded_transform, padded_shape)
    if on.sum() == 0:
        return np.zeros(shape, np.float32)
    smooth = gaussian_filter(on.astype(np.float32), sigma=sigma, mode="constant")
    return np.clip(smooth[pad_rows:pad_rows + shape[0],
                          pad_cols:pad_cols + shape[1]], 0.0, 1.0).astype(np.float32)


def compute_landuse_code(geoms, transform, shape):
    # 无数据/空 band 用 0 作为中性哨兵(与 v3 集成脚本一致); 归一化 landuse_code/8.0 时为 0.0(中性)。
    result = np.full(shape, 0, np.uint8)
    if not geoms:
        return result
    from collections import defaultdict
    groups = defaultdict(list)
    for g, tags in geoms:
        try:
            code = _landuse_tag_to_code(tags)
            groups[code].append(g)
        except Exception:
            continue
    for code in sorted(groups.keys()):
        shapes = [(g, code) for g in groups[code]]
        try:
            m = rio_rasterize(shapes, out_shape=shape, transform=transform,
                              fill=0, all_touched=True, dtype=np.uint8)
            result = np.where(m == code, code, result).astype(np.uint8)
        except Exception:
            continue
    return result


def compute_residential_landuse_mask(geoms, transform, shape):
    """Rasterize mapped ``landuse=residential`` polygons as a soft settlement proxy.

    The mask indicates mapped residential land use only. It is neither a building
    footprint/population estimate nor a legally reliable exclusion zone.
    """
    residential = [
        (geom, tags) for geom, tags in geoms
        if (tags.get("landuse") or "").strip().lower() == "residential"
    ]
    return rasterize_on(residential, transform, shape)


def load_land_mask_geojson(geojson_path, transform, shape):
    """Rasterize WGS84 land polygons as an optional hard traversability mask."""
    if not HAS_RASTERIO or not HAS_SHAPELY:
        raise RuntimeError("读取陆域 GeoJSON 需要 rasterio 和 shapely")
    path = Path(geojson_path)
    if not path.is_file():
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"陆域 GeoJSON 不存在: {path}")
    with path.open(encoding="utf-8-sig") as source_file:
        document = json.load(source_file)
    if (not isinstance(document, dict)
            or document.get("type") != "FeatureCollection"
            or not isinstance(document.get("features"), list)
            or not document["features"]):
        raise ValueError("陆域文件必须是包含要素的 WGS84 GeoJSON FeatureCollection")
    declared_crs = document.get("crs")
    if declared_crs:
        if not isinstance(declared_crs, dict):
            raise ValueError("陆域 GeoJSON 的 crs 声明无效")
        crs_properties = declared_crs.get("properties") or {}
        if not isinstance(crs_properties, dict):
            raise ValueError("陆域 GeoJSON 的 crs 声明无效")
        crs_name = str(crs_properties.get("name", "")).strip()
        try:
            parsed_crs = rasterio.crs.CRS.from_user_input(crs_name)
        except (TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
            raise ValueError("陆域 GeoJSON 必须使用 WGS84 经纬度坐标") from exc
        if (parsed_crs.to_epsg() != 4326
                and parsed_crs.to_string().upper() != "OGC:CRS84"):
            raise ValueError("陆域 GeoJSON 必须使用 WGS84 经纬度坐标")

    from shapely.geometry import shape as shapely_shape

    land_shapes = []
    repaired_feature_indices = []
    for index, feature in enumerate(document["features"], start=1):
        if not isinstance(feature, dict) or not feature.get("geometry"):
            raise ValueError(f"陆域要素 {index} 缺少有效 geometry")
        try:
            geometry = shapely_shape(feature["geometry"])
        except (TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
            raise ValueError(f"陆域要素 {index} 的 geometry 格式无效") from exc
        if (geometry.is_empty
                or geometry.geom_type not in {"Polygon", "MultiPolygon"}):
            raise ValueError(f"陆域要素 {index} 必须是有效的 Polygon 或 MultiPolygon")
        if not geometry.is_valid:
            if (geometry.geom_type != "MultiPolygon"
                    or not all(part.is_valid for part in geometry.geoms)):
                raise ValueError(f"陆域要素 {index} 必须是有效的 Polygon 或 MultiPolygon")
            geometry = unary_union(list(geometry.geoms))
            if (geometry.is_empty or not geometry.is_valid
                    or geometry.geom_type not in {"Polygon", "MultiPolygon"}):
                raise ValueError(f"陆域要素 {index} 的重叠多边形无法安全合并")
            repaired_feature_indices.append(index)
        min_lon, min_lat, max_lon, max_lat = geometry.bounds
        if (not all(math.isfinite(value) for value in geometry.bounds)
                or min_lon < -180 or max_lon > 180
                or min_lat < -90 or max_lat > 90):
            raise ValueError(f"陆域要素 {index} 坐标超出 WGS84 经纬度范围")
        land_shapes.append((geometry, 1))

    land_mask = rio_rasterize(
        land_shapes, out_shape=shape, transform=transform,
        fill=0, all_touched=False, dtype=np.uint8,
    )
    land_cell_count = int(np.count_nonzero(land_mask))
    if land_cell_count == 0:
        raise ValueError("陆域多边形没有覆盖当前规划网格")
    metadata = {
        "path": str(path.resolve()),
        "status": "loaded",
        "feature_count": len(land_shapes),
        "repaired_feature_count": len(repaired_feature_indices),
        "repaired_feature_indices": repaired_feature_indices,
        "land_cell_count": land_cell_count,
        "rasterization": "pixel_center_inside",
        "boundary_semantics": "administrative_land_polygon_screen_not_surveyed_shoreline",
    }
    if isinstance(document.get("metadata"), dict):
        metadata["dataset_metadata"] = document["metadata"]
    if isinstance(document.get("data_sources"), list):
        metadata["data_sources"] = document["data_sources"]
    return {"land_mask": land_mask, "metadata": metadata}


def load_user_constraint_overlays(geojson_path, transform, shape, *,
                                  require_verified_hard_constraints=False):
    """Rasterize user-supplied WGS84 polygon constraints into hard and soft layers."""
    if not HAS_RASTERIO or not HAS_SHAPELY:
        raise RuntimeError("读取自定义约束图层需要 rasterio 和 shapely")
    path = Path(geojson_path)
    if not path.is_file():
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"自定义约束 GeoJSON 不存在: {path}")
    with path.open(encoding="utf-8-sig") as source_file:
        document = json.load(source_file)
    if not isinstance(document, dict) or document.get("type") != "FeatureCollection" \
            or not isinstance(document.get("features"), list):
        raise ValueError("自定义约束文件必须是 WGS84 GeoJSON FeatureCollection")
    dataset_metadata = document.get("metadata")
    data_sources = document.get("data_sources")
    if dataset_metadata is not None and not isinstance(dataset_metadata, dict):
        raise ValueError("自定义约束 GeoJSON 的 metadata 必须是对象")
    if data_sources is not None and not isinstance(data_sources, list):
        raise ValueError("自定义约束 GeoJSON 的 data_sources 必须是数组")
    declared_crs = document.get("crs")
    if declared_crs:
        if not isinstance(declared_crs, dict):
            raise ValueError("自定义约束 GeoJSON 的 crs 声明无效")
        crs_properties = declared_crs.get("properties") or {}
        if not isinstance(crs_properties, dict):
            raise ValueError("自定义约束 GeoJSON 的 crs 声明无效")
        crs_name = str(crs_properties.get("name", "")).upper()
        if "4326" not in crs_name and "CRS84" not in crs_name:
            raise ValueError("自定义约束 GeoJSON 必须使用 WGS84 经纬度坐标")
    if not document["features"]:
        raise ValueError("自定义约束 GeoJSON 不包含要素")

    from shapely.geometry import shape as shapely_shape

    hard_shapes, soft_shapes = [], []
    hard_constraint_evidence = []
    source_shapes = {}
    sources, categories = set(), set()
    for index, feature in enumerate(document["features"], start=1):
        if not isinstance(feature, dict):
            raise ValueError(f"约束要素 {index} 格式无效")
        properties = feature.get("properties") or {}
        if not isinstance(properties, dict):
            raise ValueError(f"约束要素 {index} 的 properties 格式无效")
        mode = str(properties.get("mode", "")).strip().lower()
        source = str(properties.get("source", "")).strip()
        if mode not in {"hard", "soft"}:
            raise ValueError(f"约束要素 {index} 的 mode 必须是 hard 或 soft")
        if not source:
            raise ValueError(f"约束要素 {index} 缺少 source 来源字段")
        geometry_data = feature.get("geometry")
        if not geometry_data:
            raise ValueError(f"约束要素 {index} 缺少 geometry")
        try:
            geometry = shapely_shape(geometry_data)
        except (TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
            raise ValueError(f"约束要素 {index} 的 geometry 格式无效") from exc
        if geometry.is_empty or not geometry.is_valid or geometry.geom_type not in {"Polygon", "MultiPolygon"}:
            raise ValueError(f"约束要素 {index} 必须是有效的 Polygon 或 MultiPolygon")
        min_lon, min_lat, max_lon, max_lat = geometry.bounds
        if min_lon < -180 or max_lon > 180 or min_lat < -90 or max_lat > 90:
            raise ValueError(f"约束要素 {index} 坐标超出 WGS84 经纬度范围")
        if mode == "hard":
            verification_status = str(
                properties.get("verification_status", "")).strip().casefold()
            verification_reference = str(
                properties.get("verification_reference", "")).strip()
            hard_constraint_evidence.append({
                "feature_index": index,
                "source": source,
                "verification_status": verification_status or "unknown",
                "verification_reference": verification_reference or None,
            })
            if (require_verified_hard_constraints
                    and (verification_status != "verified" or not verification_reference)):
                raise ValueError(
                    f"硬约束要素 {index} 必须声明 verification_status=verified "
                    "并提供 verification_reference 才能选择塔位图方案")
            hard_shapes.append((geometry, 1))
        else:
            try:
                weight = float(properties["weight"])
            except (KeyError, TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
                raise ValueError(f"软约束要素 {index} 必须提供非负数值 weight") from exc
            if not math.isfinite(weight) or weight < 0:
                raise ValueError(f"软约束要素 {index} 的 weight 必须是非负有限数值")
            soft_shapes.append((geometry, weight))
        source_layer_shapes = source_shapes.setdefault(source, {"hard": [], "soft": []})
        source_layer_shapes[mode].append((geometry, 1 if mode == "hard" else weight))
        sources.add(source)
        category = str(properties.get("category", "")).strip()
        if category:
            categories.add(category)

    if require_verified_hard_constraints and not hard_shapes:
        raise ValueError("选择塔位图方案至少需要一个带核验凭证的硬约束要素")

    height, width = shape
    hard_mask = rio_rasterize(
        hard_shapes, out_shape=(height, width), transform=transform,
        fill=0, all_touched=True, dtype=np.uint8,
    ) if hard_shapes else np.zeros((height, width), dtype=np.uint8)
    if require_verified_hard_constraints and not np.any(hard_mask):
        raise ValueError("核验硬约束要素没有覆盖当前规划网格")
    soft_cost = rio_rasterize(
        soft_shapes, out_shape=(height, width), transform=transform,
        fill=0, all_touched=True, dtype=np.float32,
        merge_alg=rasterio.enums.MergeAlg.add,
    ) if soft_shapes else np.zeros((height, width), dtype=np.float32)
    source_layers = []
    for source, modes in sorted(source_shapes.items()):
        for mode, geometries in modes.items():
            if not geometries:
                continue
            coverage = rio_rasterize(
                [(geometry, 1) for geometry, _ in geometries],
                out_shape=(height, width), transform=transform,
                fill=0, all_touched=True, dtype=np.uint8,
            )
            covered_cell_count = int(np.count_nonzero(coverage))
            affected_cell_count = covered_cell_count
            if mode == "soft":
                del coverage
                positive_weight_shapes = [
                    (geometry, 1) for geometry, weight in geometries if weight > 0
                ]
                if positive_weight_shapes:
                    affected = rio_rasterize(
                        positive_weight_shapes, out_shape=(height, width), transform=transform,
                        fill=0, all_touched=True, dtype=np.uint8,
                    )
                    affected_cell_count = int(np.count_nonzero(affected))
                else:
                    affected_cell_count = 0
            source_layers.append({
                "source": source,
                "mode": mode,
                "feature_count": len(geometries),
                "covered_cell_count": covered_cell_count,
                "affected_cell_count": affected_cell_count,
            })
    metadata = {
        "path": str(path.resolve()),
        "status": "loaded",
        "feature_count": len(document["features"]),
        "hard_feature_count": len(hard_shapes),
        "soft_feature_count": len(soft_shapes),
        "hard_constraint_evidence": hard_constraint_evidence,
        "hard_constraint_declarations_complete": bool(hard_shapes) and all(
            item["verification_status"] == "verified"
            and item["verification_reference"]
            for item in hard_constraint_evidence),
        "hard_constraint_evidence_validation_scope": "caller_declarations_only",
        "sources": sorted(sources),
        "categories": sorted(categories),
        "hard_cell_count": int(hard_mask.sum()),
        "soft_cell_count": int(np.count_nonzero(soft_cost)),
        "source_layers": source_layers,
    }
    if dataset_metadata is not None:
        metadata["dataset_metadata"] = dataset_metadata
    if data_sources is not None:
        metadata["data_sources"] = data_sources
    return {"hard_mask": hard_mask, "soft_cost": soft_cost, "metadata": metadata}


def compute_vegetation_height(geoms, transform, shape):
    """从植被/林地类 OSM 多边形推导植被高度代理 (m)。"""
    result = np.zeros(shape, np.float32)
    if not geoms:
        return result
    from collections import defaultdict
    groups = defaultdict(list)
    for g, tags in geoms:
        lu = (tags.get("landuse") or "").lower()
        nat = (tags.get("natural") or "").lower()
        if "wood" in nat or "forest" in nat or "tree" in nat:
            h = 20.0
        elif "scrub" in nat or "grass" in nat or "grassland" in nat or "heath" in nat:
            h = 5.0
        elif "wetland" in nat or "marsh" in nat:
            h = 2.0
        elif "forest" in lu or "wood" in lu:
            h = 20.0
        else:
            h = 3.0
        groups[h].append(g)
    for h, glist in groups.items():
        m = rasterize_on([(g, {}) for g in glist], transform, shape)
        result = np.where(m > 0, h, result).astype(np.float32)
    return result


def compute_osm_bands(dem, transform, bbox, cache_dir=None, *,
                      include_model_features=True, include_building_distance=True):
    """计算 OSM 波段与风险代理；图层状态可从返回值的 ``metadata`` 属性读取。

    Building footprints provide a hard footprint mask and settlement proxy;
    residential land use provides a soft settlement proxy and model feature.
    Other layers are model features only and are not used by the default rule
    cost. Protected areas are reported for inspection only.
    """
    H, W = dem.shape
    cache_dir = Path(cache_dir) if cache_dir else DOWNLOADED_DIR
    layer_specs = {
        "roads": ("taiwan_roads.pkl", "line", "feature_model_input_only_not_default_rule_cost"),
        "water": ("taiwan_water.pkl", "line", "feature_model_input_only_not_default_rule_cost"),
        "railways": ("taiwan_railways.pkl", "line", "feature_model_input_only_not_default_rule_cost"),
        "faults": ("taiwan_faults.pkl", "line", "feature_model_input_only_not_default_rule_cost"),
        "landuse": ("taiwan_landuse.pkl", "polygon", "settlement_and_optional_landcover_soft_proxy"),
        "buildings": ("taiwan_buildings.pkl", "polygon", "hard_footprint_and_settlement_proxy"),
        "vegetation": ("taiwan_vegetation.pkl", "polygon", "feature_model_input_only_not_default_rule_cost"),
        "protected_areas": ("taiwan_protected.pkl", "polygon", "informational_only"),
    }
    layers = {}
    layer_metadata = {}
    for name, (filename, geom_type, role) in layer_specs.items():
        if not include_model_features and name not in ("landuse", "buildings"):
            layers[name] = []
            layer_metadata[name] = {
                "path": str(cache_dir / filename), "status": "not_loaded",
                "reason": "not_required_by_selected_planner_mode",
                "coverage_status": "not_checked", "planning_role": role,
            }
            continue
        query_bbox = bbox
        if name == "buildings":
            latitude = 0.5 * (bbox[1] + bbox[3])
            buffer_m = 5000.0 if include_building_distance else 2000.0
            lat_buffer = buffer_m / 111320.0
            lon_buffer = lat_buffer / max(math.cos(math.radians(latitude)), 1e-6)
            query_bbox = (bbox[0] - lon_buffer, bbox[1] - lat_buffer,
                          bbox[2] + lon_buffer, bbox[3] + lat_buffer)
        layers[name], layer_metadata[name] = load_osm_geoms_with_status(
            cache_dir / filename, geom_type, query_bbox)
        layer_metadata[name]["planning_role"] = role
    roads = layers["roads"]
    water = layers["water"]
    railways = layers["railways"]
    faults = layers["faults"]
    landuse = layers["landuse"]
    buildings = layers["buildings"]
    vegetation = layers["vegetation"]
    if include_model_features:
        print(f"  OSM 几何数: 道路{len(roads)} 水域{len(water)} 铁路{len(railways)} "
              f"断裂{len(faults)} 土地利用{len(landuse)} 建筑{len(buildings)} 植被{len(vegetation)}")
    else:
        print(f"  OSM 规划图层: 土地利用{len(landuse)} 建筑{len(buildings)}；"
              "未加载仅供模型或诊断的可选图层")

    bands = OSMBands(metadata={"layers": layer_metadata},
                     geometries={"buildings": buildings})
    if include_model_features:
        bands["dist_road"] = compute_distance_band(roads, transform, (H, W), 5000.0)
        bands["dist_water"] = compute_distance_band(water, transform, (H, W), 5000.0)
        bands["dist_railway"] = compute_distance_band(railways, transform, (H, W), 5000.0)
        bands["dist_fault"] = compute_distance_band(faults, transform, (H, W), 10000.0)
    bands["landuse_code"] = compute_landuse_code(landuse, transform, (H, W))
    bands["residential_landuse_mask"] = compute_residential_landuse_mask(landuse, transform, (H, W))
    bands["building_density"] = compute_building_density(buildings, transform, (H, W))
    bands["building_mask"] = rasterize_on(buildings, transform, (H, W))
    if include_building_distance:
        bands["building_distance_m"] = compute_distance_band(buildings, transform, (H, W), 5000.0)
    if include_model_features:
        bands["vegetation_height"] = compute_vegetation_height(vegetation, transform, (H, W))

    if include_model_features:
        # 风险代理波段（21-25）— 由地形/位置推导
        z = _fill(dem).astype(np.float32)
        lat_center = transform.f + transform.e * (H / 2.0)  # 窗口纬度中心（修正：之前误用 0）
        row_m, col_m = pixel_spacing_meters(transform, lat_center)
        dy, dx = _terrain_gradient(z, row_m, col_m)
        slope = np.degrees(np.arctan(np.sqrt(dx ** 2 + dy ** 2)))
        aspect = np.degrees(np.arctan2(-dy, dx)) % 360
        elev_n = np.clip(z / 3000.0, 0, 1)
        slope_n = np.clip(slope / 45.0, 0, 1)
        # 台风：东南坡 + 低海拔 + 陡坡
        aspect_score = np.where((aspect >= 45) & (aspect < 225), 1.0, 0.3)
        bands["typhoon_risk"] = np.clip(0.4 * slope_n + 0.35 * aspect_score + 0.25 * (1 - elev_n), 0, 1).astype(np.float32)
        # 地震：基础 0.6 + 坡度
        bands["seismic_risk"] = np.clip(0.6 + 0.3 * slope_n, 0, 1).astype(np.float32)
        # 滑坡：坡度 + 粗糙度
        rough = np.nan_to_num(ndi.generic_filter(z, np.nanstd, size=3), nan=0.0) if HAS_SCIPY else np.zeros_like(z)
        bands["landslide_risk"] = np.clip(0.5 * slope_n + 0.3 * np.clip(rough / 200.0, 0, 1), 0, 1).astype(np.float32)
        # 覆冰：高海拔
        bands["ice_cover_risk"] = np.clip((z - 2000) / 2000.0, 0, 1).astype(np.float32)
        # 雷击：坡度 + 海拔
        bands["lightning_risk"] = np.clip(0.6 * slope_n + 0.4 * elev_n, 0, 1).astype(np.float32)
    return bands
# ===== transmission_path_planner/provenance.py =====
"""Run provenance for reproducible route-planning benchmarks."""

from datetime import datetime, timezone
import hashlib
from importlib import metadata
import json
import platform
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess
import sys


OSM_CACHE_FILES = {
    "roads": "taiwan_roads.pkl",
    "water": "taiwan_water.pkl",
    "railways": "taiwan_railways.pkl",
    "faults": "taiwan_faults.pkl",
    "landuse": "taiwan_landuse.pkl",
    "buildings": "taiwan_buildings.pkl",
    "vegetation": "taiwan_vegetation.pkl",
    "protected_areas": "taiwan_protected.pkl",
}
DEPENDENCIES = (
    "numpy", "scipy", "pandas", "shapely", "rasterio", "geopandas",
    "pyproj", "pyogrio", "matplotlib", "torch", "osmnx",
)
PROVENANCE_FILENAME = "run_provenance.json"


def _utc_now():
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(timespec="seconds")


def _json_value(value):
    if isinstance(value, Path):
        return str(value)
    if isinstance(value, dict):
        return {str(key): _json_value(item) for key, item in value.items()}
    if isinstance(value, (list, tuple)):
        return [_json_value(item) for item in value]
    if value is None or isinstance(value, (str, int, float, bool)):
        return value
    return str(value)


def sha256_file(path):
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with Path(path).open("rb") as stream:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: stream.read(1024 * 1024), b""):
            digest.update(chunk)
    return digest.hexdigest()


def _file_record(role, path):
    path = Path(path).resolve()
    record = {"role": role, "path": str(path)}
    if not path.is_file():
        record["status"] = "missing"
        return record
    try:
        record.update(
            status="hashed",
            size_bytes=path.stat().st_size,
            sha256=sha256_file(path),
        )
    except OSError as exc:
        record.update(status="unreadable", error=str(exc))
    return record


def _arg(args, name, default=None):
    if isinstance(args, dict):
        return args.get(name, default)
    return getattr(args, name, default)


def _planning_input_records(args, dem_path, default_osm_cache_dir, model_weights,
                            has_torch):
    records = []
    for name, role in (
        ("stations_csv", "station_table"),
        ("route_tasks_csv", "route_tasks"),
        ("real_cases_csv", "case_tasks"),
        ("constraint_geojson", "user_constraints"),
        ("land_mask_geojson", "land_mask"),
        ("population_raster", "population_raster"),
    ):
        path = _arg(args, name)
        if path:
            records.append(_file_record(role, path))
    records.append(_file_record("dem", dem_path))

    cache_dir = Path(_arg(args, "osm_cache_dir") or default_osm_cache_dir)
    include_model_features = bool(
        _arg(args, "include_ai_diagnostics") and has_torch
        and not _arg(args, "no_vin"))
    layer_names = list(OSM_CACHE_FILES)
    if not include_model_features:
        layer_names = ["landuse", "buildings"]
    for layer_name in layer_names:
        records.append(_file_record(
            f"osm_cache:{layer_name}", cache_dir / OSM_CACHE_FILES[layer_name]))

    hazard_dir = Path(_arg(args, "official_hazards_dir"))
    if hazard_dir.is_dir():
        for fixed_name in ("manifest.json", "tiles.sha256"):
            path = hazard_dir / fixed_name
            if path.is_file():
                records.append(_file_record("official_hazard_manifest", path))
        for path in sorted(hazard_dir.rglob("*.pbf")):
            if path.is_file() and not path.is_symlink():
                records.append(_file_record("official_hazard_tile", path))
    else:
        records.append({
            "role": "official_hazards_dir",
            "path": str(hazard_dir.resolve()),
            "status": "missing",
        })

    if _arg(args, "use_costunet") and has_torch:
        for path in model_weights:
            if Path(path).is_file():
                records.append(_file_record("costunet_weights", path))
    return records


def _source_state(repo_path):
    def git(*arguments, cwd=None):
        return subprocess.run(
            ["git", "-C", str(cwd or repo_path), *arguments],
            check=False, capture_output=True, text=True, encoding="utf-8",
            errors="replace",
        )

    try:
        root = git("rev-parse", "--show-toplevel")
        if root.returncode != 0:
            return {"available": False,
                    "reason": root.stderr.strip() or "not a Git worktree"}
        git_root = Path(root.stdout.strip())
        commit = git("rev-parse", "HEAD", cwd=git_root)
        branch = git("branch", "--show-current", cwd=git_root)
        status = git("status", "--porcelain=v1", "--untracked-files=normal", cwd=git_root)
    except OSError as exc:
        return {"available": False, "reason": str(exc)}
    if commit.returncode != 0 or status.returncode != 0:
        return {"available": False, "reason": "unable to read Git state"}
    entries = [line for line in status.stdout.splitlines() if line]
    package_dir = Path(repo_path).resolve() / "transmission_path_planner"
    if not package_dir.is_dir():
        package_dir = git_root / "transmission_path_planner"
    source_files = []
    if package_dir.is_dir():
        for path in sorted(package_dir.rglob("*.py")):
            if "__pycache__" in path.parts or path.is_symlink():
                continue
            source_files.append({
                "path": path.relative_to(git_root).as_posix(),
                "size_bytes": path.stat().st_size,
                "sha256": sha256_file(path),
            })
    return {
        "available": True,
        "root": str(git_root),
        "commit": commit.stdout.strip(),
        "branch": branch.stdout.strip() or None,
        "dirty": bool(entries),
        "status_porcelain": entries,
        "runtime_source_files": source_files,
    }


def _runtime_info():
    versions = {}
    for package in DEPENDENCIES:
        try:
            versions[package] = metadata.version(package)
        except metadata.PackageNotFoundError:
            versions[package] = None
    return {
        "python_version": sys.version,
        "python_executable": sys.executable,
        "implementation": platform.python_implementation(),
        "platform": platform.platform(),
        "dependencies": versions,
    }


def _case_records(cases):
    return [
        {
            "case": name,
            "start_latlon": _json_value(start),
            "end_latlon": _json_value(end),
        }
        for name, start, end in cases
    ]


def capture_run_provenance(repo_path, args, cases, dem_path,
                           default_osm_cache_dir, model_weights=(),
                           has_torch=True, effective_runtime=None):
    """Capture run configuration and planning inputs before route generation.

    Reference geometry paths are recorded as configuration only; this function
    never opens, hashes, or walks ``real_dir`` or ``real_shp``.
    """
    configuration = _json_value(vars(args) if hasattr(args, "__dict__") else args)
    return {
        "schema_version": 1,
        "started_at_utc": _utc_now(),
        "command": {"argv": list(sys.argv), "cwd": str(Path.cwd())},
        "configuration": configuration,
        "cases": _case_records(cases),
        "source": _source_state(repo_path),
        "runtime": _runtime_info(),
        "effective_runtime": _json_value(
            effective_runtime or {"has_torch": has_torch}),
        "planning_inputs": _planning_input_records(
            args, dem_path, default_osm_cache_dir, model_weights, has_torch),
        "reference_geometry_policy": {
            "hashed_by_provenance": False,
            "paths_are_configuration_only": True,
        },
    }


def _is_within(path, parent):
    try:
        path.relative_to(parent)
        return True
    except ValueError:
        return False


def _output_records(output_dir, excluded_reference_paths=()):
    root = Path(output_dir).resolve()
    excluded = [Path(path).resolve() for path in excluded_reference_paths if path]
    records = []
    for path in sorted(root.rglob("*")):
        if not path.is_file() or path.is_symlink() or path.name == PROVENANCE_FILENAME:
            continue
        resolved = path.resolve()
        if any(resolved == item or _is_within(resolved, item) for item in excluded):
            continue
        record = {"path": path.relative_to(root).as_posix()}
        try:
            record.update(size_bytes=path.stat().st_size, sha256=sha256_file(path),
                          status="hashed")
        except OSError as exc:
            record.update(status="unreadable", error=str(exc))
        records.append(record)
    return records


def write_run_provenance(snapshot, output_dir, excluded_reference_paths=()):
    """Write the final manifest after normal outputs exist, hashing each once."""
    document = dict(snapshot)
    document["completed_at_utc"] = _utc_now()
    document["output_directory"] = str(Path(output_dir).resolve())
    document["outputs"] = _output_records(output_dir, excluded_reference_paths)
    document["output_hash_policy"] = (
        "SHA-256 of regular files under output_directory, excluding this manifest "
        "and paths identified as reference inputs")
    path = Path(output_dir) / PROVENANCE_FILENAME
    path.write_text(json.dumps(document, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + "\n",
                    encoding="utf-8")
    return path
# ===== transmission_path_planner/rebuild_terrarium_dem.py =====
"""Rebuild an EPSG:4326 DEM from cached AWS Terrarium XYZ tiles."""

import argparse
import math
import os
import tempfile
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import rasterio
from PIL import Image
from rasterio.enums import Resampling
from rasterio.transform import Affine
from rasterio.warp import reproject, transform_bounds
from rasterio.windows import Window

from .terrarium import XYZ_TILE_SIZE, terrarium_rgb_to_elevation, xyz_tile_transform


def lonlat_to_xyz(lon, lat, zoom):
    n = 2 ** zoom
    lat = min(max(lat, -85.05112878), 85.05112878)
    x = math.floor((lon + 180.0) / 360.0 * n)
    y = math.floor((1.0 - math.asinh(math.tan(math.radians(lat))) / math.pi)
                   * 0.5 * n)
    return x, y


def rebuild_dem(tile_cache, output_path, bbox, zoom=12, overwrite=False):
    """Warp cached Web Mercator tiles to a regular geographic grid.

    ``bbox`` is ``(min_lon, min_lat, max_lon, max_lat)``. The source tile rows
    remain in EPSG:3857 until GDAL reprojects them; they are never interpreted
    as equally spaced latitude rows.
    """
    cache = Path(tile_cache)
    output = Path(output_path)
    if output.exists() and not overwrite:
        raise FileExistsError(f"output already exists: {output}; pass --overwrite to replace it")
    output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    min_lon, min_lat, max_lon, max_lat = map(float, bbox)
    x0, y0 = lonlat_to_xyz(min_lon, max_lat, zoom)
    x1, y1 = lonlat_to_xyz(max_lon, min_lat, zoom)
    nx, ny = x1 - x0 + 1, y1 - y0 + 1
    tile_paths = {
        (x, y): cache / f"t_{zoom}_{x}_{y}.png"
        for y in range(y0, y1 + 1)
        for x in range(x0, x1 + 1)
    }
    missing = [str(path) for path in tile_paths.values() if not path.is_file()]
    if missing:
        preview = ", ".join(missing[:5])
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"{len(missing)} XYZ tiles are missing: {preview}")

    source_transform = xyz_tile_transform(zoom, x0, y0)
    source_width, source_height = nx * XYZ_TILE_SIZE, ny * XYZ_TILE_SIZE
    source_profile = {
        "driver": "GTiff", "width": source_width, "height": source_height,
