        "count": 1, "dtype": "float32", "crs": "EPSG:3857",
        "transform": source_transform, "nodata": -32768.0,
        "compress": "lzw", "tiled": True,
        "blockxsize": XYZ_TILE_SIZE, "blockysize": XYZ_TILE_SIZE,
        "BIGTIFF": "IF_SAFER",
    }

    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="terrarium-warp-", dir=output.parent) as temp_dir:
        source_path = Path(temp_dir) / "terrarium_3857.tif"
        warped_path = Path(temp_dir) / output.name
        with rasterio.open(source_path, "w", **source_profile) as source:
            for (tile_x, tile_y), tile_path in tile_paths.items():
                with Image.open(tile_path) as tile_image:
                    rgba = np.asarray(tile_image.convert("RGBA"))
                values = terrarium_rgb_to_elevation(rgba)
                invalid = ((rgba[..., 3] == 0) | (values < -100.0) | (values > 9000.0))
                values[invalid] = source_profile["nodata"]
                source.write(values, 1, window=Window(
                    (tile_x - x0) * XYZ_TILE_SIZE,
                    (tile_y - y0) * XYZ_TILE_SIZE,
                    XYZ_TILE_SIZE, XYZ_TILE_SIZE,
                ))
            source.update_tags(
                source="AWS Terrain Tiles Terrarium XYZ cache",
                zoom=str(zoom),
                tile_range=f"x={x0}-{x1};y={y0}-{y1}",
                elevation_encoding="R*256 + G + B/256 - 32768 metres",
                vertical_datum="not verified from the cached PNG files",
            )

        with rasterio.open(source_path) as source:
            left, bottom, right, top = transform_bounds(
                source.crs, "EPSG:4326", *source.bounds, densify_pts=21)
            center_lat = 0.5 * (bottom + top)
            xres = 360.0 / (XYZ_TILE_SIZE * (2 ** zoom))
            yres = xres * math.cos(math.radians(center_lat))
            width = math.ceil((right - left) / xres)
            height = math.ceil((top - bottom) / yres)
            destination_transform = Affine(xres, 0.0, left, 0.0, -yres, top)
            destination_profile = {
                "driver": "GTiff", "width": width, "height": height,
                "count": 1, "dtype": "float32", "crs": "EPSG:4326",
                "transform": destination_transform, "nodata": -32768.0,
                "compress": "lzw", "tiled": True,
                "blockxsize": XYZ_TILE_SIZE, "blockysize": XYZ_TILE_SIZE,
                "BIGTIFF": "IF_SAFER",
            }
            with rasterio.open(warped_path, "w", **destination_profile) as destination:
                reproject(
                    source=rasterio.band(source, 1),
                    destination=rasterio.band(destination, 1),
                    src_transform=source.transform, src_crs=source.crs,
                    src_nodata=source.nodata,
                    dst_transform=destination_transform, dst_crs="EPSG:4326",
                    dst_nodata=destination_profile["nodata"],
                    resampling=Resampling.bilinear, num_threads=2,
                )
                destination.update_tags(
                    source="AWS Terrain Tiles Terrarium XYZ cache",
                    source_crs="EPSG:3857",
                    source_zoom=str(zoom),
                    source_tile_range=f"x={x0}-{x1};y={y0}-{y1}",
                    georeferencing="Web Mercator XYZ tiles reprojected to EPSG:4326",
                    resampling="bilinear",
                    vertical_datum="not verified from the cached PNG files",
                )

        os.replace(warped_path, output)

    with rasterio.open(output) as result:
        print(f"Wrote {output}: {result.width}x{result.height}, "
              f"EPSG:4326, bounds={tuple(round(v, 6) for v in result.bounds)}")


def main():
    project_dir = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("--tile-cache", required=True, help="directory containing t_Z_X_Y.png")
    parser.add_argument("--output", default=str(project_dir / "data" / "taiwan_dem_35m_georef_fixed.tif"))
    parser.add_argument("--bbox", nargs=4, type=float, default=(119.4, 21.7, 122.3, 25.5),
                        metavar=("MIN_LON", "MIN_LAT", "MAX_LON", "MAX_LAT"))
    parser.add_argument("--zoom", type=int, default=12)
    parser.add_argument("--overwrite", action="store_true")
    args = parser.parse_args()
    rebuild_dem(args.tile_cache, args.output, args.bbox, args.zoom, args.overwrite)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
# ===== transmission_path_planner/smoothing.py =====
# transmission_path_planner/smoothing.py
"""路径平滑、硬性约束吸附、高程采样。"""

from .config import *
from .geometry import *

def _rdp(points, epsilon):
    """Ramer-Douglas-Peucker 折线简化。

    改用显式栈的迭代实现, 避免对长且高度弯曲的路径递归过深触发
    'maximum recursion depth exceeded' (Case 3 PPO 路径点数常达数千, 单边反复
    细分时递归深度可超 1000)。迭代版结果与递归版完全一致。
    """
    points = list(points)
    n = len(points)
    if n < 3:
        return points
    keep = [False] * n
    keep[0] = True
    keep[n - 1] = True
    stack = [(0, n - 1)]
    while stack:
        s_i, e_i = stack.pop()
        if e_i <= s_i + 1:
            continue
        s = np.array(points[s_i], np.float64)
        e = np.array(points[e_i], np.float64)
        lv = e - s
        ll = np.linalg.norm(lv)
        if ll < 1e-12:
            # 退化段: 端点已标记保留, 丢弃内部点(与原递归版行为一致)
            continue
        unit = lv / ll
        dmax, idx = 0.0, s_i + 1
        for i in range(s_i + 1, e_i):
            v = np.array(points[i], np.float64) - s
            proj = np.clip(np.dot(v, unit), 0, ll)
            closest = s + proj * unit
            d = np.linalg.norm(np.array(points[i], np.float64) - closest)
            if d > dmax:
                dmax, idx = d, i
        if dmax > epsilon:
            keep[idx] = True
            stack.append((s_i, idx))
            stack.append((idx, e_i))
    return [points[i] for i in range(n) if keep[i]]


def smooth_path(geo, spacing_m=90.0):
    if len(geo) < 3:
        return geo
    arr = np.array([(lon, lat) for lon, lat in geo], np.float64)
    from pyproj import CRS, Transformer

    lons = np.radians(arr[:, 0])
    lon0 = math.degrees(math.atan2(float(np.sin(lons).mean()), float(np.cos(lons).mean())))
    lat0 = float(np.mean(arr[:, 1]))
    local_crs = CRS.from_proj4(
        f"+proj=aeqd +lat_0={lat0} +lon_0={lon0} +datum=WGS84 +units=m +no_defs"
    )
    project = Transformer.from_crs("EPSG:4326", local_crs, always_xy=True).transform
    unproject = Transformer.from_crs(local_crs, "EPSG:4326", always_xy=True).transform
    metric = np.asarray([project(lon, lat) for lon, lat in arr], dtype=np.float64)
    epsilon_m = min(max(float(spacing_m), 0.0) * 0.5, 50.0)
    simplified = _rdp(metric.tolist(), epsilon_m)
    if len(simplified) < 2:
        return geo
    simplified = np.array(simplified)
    xs, ys = simplified[:, 0], simplified[:, 1]
    seg = np.sqrt(np.diff(xs) ** 2 + np.diff(ys) ** 2)
    cum = np.concatenate([[0], np.cumsum(seg)])
    total = cum[-1]
    if total < 1e-9:
        return geo
    n = max(int(total / max(float(spacing_m), 1e-6)), len(simplified))
    sd = np.linspace(0, total, n)
    xi = np.interp(sd, cum, xs); yi = np.interp(sd, cum, ys)
    result = [tuple(map(float, unproject(xi[i], yi[i]))) for i in range(n)]
    result[0], result[-1] = tuple(geo[0]), tuple(geo[-1])
    return result


def snap_to_valid(geo, transform, hard_mask, max_offset_m=100.0):
    """把平滑后的 geo 路径中落到硬约束屏蔽格(陡坡/超高/无效DEM)的点, 吸附到最近的合法格中心。
    推理 rollout 只走合法邻居, 原始格路径已是 0 违规; 但 smooth_path 在地理空间等距重采样时会
    越过硬约束格。吸附距离使用米制上限, 超出范围则拒绝候选并由调用方回退到原始路径。"""
    H, W = hard_mask.shape
    center_lat = transform.f + transform.e * (H / 2.0)
    row_m, col_m = pixel_spacing_meters(transform, center_lat)
    max_cells = int(math.ceil(max(float(max_offset_m), 0.0) / min(row_m, col_m)))
    out = []
    for lon, lat in geo:
        r, c = geo_to_rowcol(lat, lon, transform)
        r = int(round(r)); c = int(round(c))
        if 0 <= r < H and 0 <= c < W and hard_mask[r, c] == 1:
            out.append((lon, lat)); continue
        candidates = []
        for dr in range(-max_cells, max_cells + 1):
            for dc in range(-max_cells, max_cells + 1):
                rr, cc = r + dr, c + dc
                if not (0 <= rr < H and 0 <= cc < W) or hard_mask[rr, cc] != 1:
                    continue
                distance_m = haversine_km(lon, lat, *rowcol_to_geo(rr, cc, transform)) * 1000.0
                if distance_m <= max_offset_m:
                    candidates.append((distance_m, rr, cc))
        if not candidates:
            return None
        _, rr, cc = min(candidates)
        out.append(rowcol_to_geo(rr, cc, transform))
    return out


def _grid_cells_touched(c0, r0, c1, r1):
    """Return every raster cell touched by a segment in grid-corner coordinates."""
    dx, dy = c1 - c0, r1 - r0
    cells = set()

    def axis_cells(value):
        nearest = round(value)
        if abs(value - nearest) <= 1e-10:
            return (int(nearest) - 1, int(nearest))
        return (int(math.floor(value)),)

    def add_touching(x, y):
        for row in axis_cells(y):
            for col in axis_cells(x):
                cells.add((row, col))

    add_touching(c0, r0)
    add_touching(c1, r1)

    sx = 1 if dx > 0 else -1 if dx < 0 else 0
    sy = 1 if dy > 0 else -1 if dy < 0 else 0
    col, row = int(math.floor(c0)), int(math.floor(r0))
    if sx:
        t_max_x = ((col + 1 - c0) if sx > 0 else (c0 - col)) / abs(dx)
        t_delta_x = 1.0 / abs(dx)
    else:
        t_max_x = t_delta_x = math.inf
    if sy:
        t_max_y = ((row + 1 - r0) if sy > 0 else (r0 - row)) / abs(dy)
        t_delta_y = 1.0 / abs(dy)
    else:
        t_max_y = t_delta_y = math.inf

    while min(t_max_x, t_max_y) <= 1.0 + 1e-12:
        if abs(t_max_x - t_max_y) <= 1e-12:
            cells.add((row, col + sx))
            cells.add((row + sy, col))
            col += sx
            row += sy
            cells.add((row, col))
            t_max_x += t_delta_x
            t_max_y += t_delta_y
        elif t_max_x < t_max_y:
            col += sx
            cells.add((row, col))
            t_max_x += t_delta_x
        else:
            row += sy
            cells.add((row, col))
            t_max_y += t_delta_y

    if sx == 0 and abs(c0 - round(c0)) <= 1e-10:
        boundary_col = int(round(c0))
        cells.update((row, boundary_col - 1) for row, col in tuple(cells)
                     if col == boundary_col)
    if sy == 0 and abs(r0 - round(r0)) <= 1e-10:
        boundary_row = int(round(r0))
        cells.update((boundary_row - 1, col) for row, col in tuple(cells)
                     if row == boundary_row)
    return cells


def path_hard_violations(geo, transform, hard_mask):
    """Count blocked/out-of-grid cells crossed by a path, including segment interiors."""
    if not geo:
        return 0
    inv = ~transform
    cells = set()
    grid_points = [inv * point for point in geo]
    for (c0, r0), (c1, r1) in zip(grid_points, grid_points[1:]):
        cells.update(_grid_cells_touched(c0, r0, c1, r1))
    if len(grid_points) == 1:
        c, r = grid_points[0]
        cells.update(_grid_cells_touched(c, r, c, r))
    return sum(1 for r, c in cells
               if not (0 <= r < hard_mask.shape[0] and 0 <= c < hard_mask.shape[1])
               or hard_mask[r, c] == 0)


def _ground_grade_segment_cells(c0, r0, c1, r1, dem):
    """Sample the DEM cells along one segment using the final route validator's rule."""
    H, W = dem.shape
    steps = max(1, int(math.ceil(4 * max(abs(r1 - r0), abs(c1 - c0)))))
    cells = []
    for i in range(steps + 1):
        t = i / steps
        r, c = r0 + (r1 - r0) * t, c0 + (c1 - c0) * t
        rr, cc = int(math.floor(r)), int(math.floor(c))
        cell = ((rr, cc) if 0 <= rr < H and 0 <= cc < W
                and np.isfinite(dem[rr, cc]) else None)
        if not cells or cell != cells[-1]:
            cells.append(cell)
    return cells


def _ground_grade_cells_report(cells, dem, row_spacing_m, col_spacing_m,
                               max_ground_grade):
    violations = 0
    unassessed = 0
    max_ratio = 0.0
    for first, second in zip(cells, cells[1:]):
        if first is None or second is None:
            unassessed += 1
            continue
        r0, c0 = first
        r1, c1 = second
        if first == second:
            continue
        distance_m = math.hypot((r1 - r0) * row_spacing_m,
                                (c1 - c0) * col_spacing_m)
        if distance_m <= 0:
            unassessed += 1
            continue
        ratio = abs(float(dem[r1, c1]) - float(dem[r0, c0])) / distance_m
        max_ratio = max(max_ratio, ratio)
        if ratio > max_ground_grade + 1e-9:
            violations += 1
    return violations, unassessed, max_ratio


def _ground_grade_grid_edge_passes(start_rc, end_rc, dem, pixel_spacing,
                                   max_ground_grade):
    """Check a cell-center A* edge with the same DEM sampling as route validation."""
    row0, col0 = start_rc
    row1, col1 = end_rc
    cells = _ground_grade_segment_cells(
        col0 + 0.5, row0 + 0.5, col1 + 0.5, row1 + 0.5, dem)
    violations, unassessed, _ = _ground_grade_cells_report(
        cells, dem, pixel_spacing[0], pixel_spacing[1], max_ground_grade)
    return violations == 0 and unassessed == 0


def ground_grade_limit_report(geo, dem, transform, max_ground_grade, crs=None):
    """Check consecutive DEM cells crossed by a route against a ground-grade ratio cap."""
    if max_ground_grade is None:
        return {
            "grade_limit_status": "not_configured",
            "grade_limit_violation_count": 0,
            "grade_limit_unassessed_count": 0,
            "grade_limit_max_sampled_grade_ratio": None,
            "grade_limit_threshold_ratio": None,
        }
    if not geo or len(geo) < 2:
        return {
            "grade_limit_status": "no_path",
            "grade_limit_violation_count": 0,
            "grade_limit_unassessed_count": 0,
            "grade_limit_max_sampled_grade_ratio": None,
            "grade_limit_threshold_ratio": max_ground_grade,
        }

    inv = ~transform
    lat_center = transform.f + transform.e * (dem.shape[0] / 2.0)
    row_spacing_m, col_spacing_m = pixel_spacing_meters(transform, lat_center, crs)
    cells = []
    last_cell = object()
    grid_points = [inv * point for point in geo]
    if len(grid_points) == 1:
        segments = [(grid_points[0], grid_points[0])]
    else:
        segments = zip(grid_points, grid_points[1:])
    for (c0, r0), (c1, r1) in segments:
        for marker in _ground_grade_segment_cells(c0, r0, c1, r1, dem):
            if marker != last_cell:
                cells.append(marker)
                last_cell = marker

    violations, unassessed, max_ratio = _ground_grade_cells_report(
        cells, dem, row_spacing_m, col_spacing_m, max_ground_grade)

    status = "fail" if violations or unassessed else "pass"
    return {
        "grade_limit_status": status,
        "grade_limit_violation_count": violations,
        "grade_limit_unassessed_count": unassessed,
        "grade_limit_max_sampled_grade_ratio": round(max_ratio, 6),
        "grade_limit_threshold_ratio": max_ground_grade,
    }


def filter_path_by_ground_grade(geo, dem, transform, max_ground_grade, crs=None):
    """Reject a route when a configured grade cap is violated or cannot be assessed."""
    report = ground_grade_limit_report(geo, dem, transform, max_ground_grade, crs)
    if max_ground_grade is not None and report["grade_limit_status"] != "pass":
        return None, report
    return geo, report


def smooth_path_with_constraints(geo, transform, hard_mask, cost_surface=None, dem=None,
                                grade_penalty_weight=1.0, turn_penalty_weight=0.0,
                                pixel_spacing=None, max_ground_grade=None, crs=None,
                                shortcut_endpoints=None):
    """Try a legal, non-increasing direct shortcut, then use the existing smoother."""
    if not geo:
        return None

    def grade_is_valid(path):
        return (max_ground_grade is None or (dem is not None and
                ground_grade_limit_report(
                    path, dem, transform, max_ground_grade, crs
                )["grade_limit_status"] == "pass"))

    if len(geo) >= 3 and cost_surface is not None and dem is not None:
        direct = [geo[0], geo[-1]]
        raw_for_guard = list(geo)
        direct_for_guard = direct
        if shortcut_endpoints is not None:
            start, end = shortcut_endpoints
            start = tuple(map(float, start))
            end = tuple(map(float, end))
            raw_for_guard[0], raw_for_guard[-1] = start, end
            direct_for_guard = [start, end]
        if path_hard_violations(direct_for_guard, transform, hard_mask) == 0:
            grade_routes = (
                [direct] if direct_for_guard == direct else [direct, direct_for_guard]
            )
            grade_ok = max_ground_grade is None or all(
                ground_grade_limit_report(
                    route, dem, transform, max_ground_grade, crs
                )["grade_limit_status"] == "pass"
                for route in grade_routes
            )
            if grade_ok:
                # Import at call time to avoid the metrics -> smoothing import cycle.
                from .metrics import path_cost_integral

                raw_objective = path_cost_integral(
                    raw_for_guard, dem, transform, cost_surface, grade_penalty_weight,
                    turn_penalty_weight, pixel_spacing)
                direct_objective = path_cost_integral(
                    direct_for_guard, dem, transform, cost_surface, grade_penalty_weight,
                    turn_penalty_weight, pixel_spacing)
                if (raw_objective.get("status") == "measured"
                        and direct_objective.get("status") == "measured"):
                    raw_cost = raw_objective["total_cost_meter"]
                    direct_cost = direct_objective["total_cost_meter"]
                    tolerance = max(abs(raw_cost), 1.0) * 1e-9
                    if direct_cost <= raw_cost + tolerance:
                        return direct

    candidate = snap_to_valid(smooth_path(geo), transform, hard_mask)
    if (candidate and path_hard_violations(candidate, transform, hard_mask) == 0
            and grade_is_valid(candidate)):
        if cost_surface is None:
            return candidate
        if dem is not None:
            # Import at call time to avoid the metrics -> smoothing import cycle.
            from .metrics import path_cost_integral

            raw_objective = path_cost_integral(
                geo, dem, transform, cost_surface, grade_penalty_weight,
                turn_penalty_weight, pixel_spacing)
            smooth_objective = path_cost_integral(
                candidate, dem, transform, cost_surface, grade_penalty_weight,
                turn_penalty_weight, pixel_spacing)
            if (raw_objective.get("status") == "measured"
                    and smooth_objective.get("status") == "measured"):
                raw_cost = raw_objective["total_cost_meter"]
                smooth_cost = smooth_objective["total_cost_meter"]
                tolerance = max(abs(raw_cost), 1.0) * 1e-9
                if smooth_cost <= raw_cost + tolerance:
                    return candidate
    if (path_hard_violations(geo, transform, hard_mask) == 0
            and grade_is_valid(geo)):
        return geo
    return None


def sample_dem(coords, dem, transform):
    out = []
    H, W = dem.shape
    for lon, lat in coords:
        r, c = geo_to_rowcol(lat, lon, transform)
        if 0 <= r < H and 0 <= c < W and np.isfinite(dem[r, c]):
            out.append(float(dem[r, c]))
        else:
            out.append(np.nan)
    return out


def densify_path_for_raster(coords, transform):
    """Interpolate each route segment so no step exceeds one pixel on either axis."""
    coords = list(coords or [])
    if len(coords) < 2:
        return coords
    inv = ~transform
    grid = [inv * point for point in coords]
    dense = [coords[0]]
    for i, ((c0, r0), (c1, r1)) in enumerate(zip(grid, grid[1:])):
        steps = max(1, int(math.ceil(max(abs(r1 - r0), abs(c1 - c0)))))
        x0, y0 = coords[i]
        x1, y1 = coords[i + 1]
        dense.extend((x0 + (x1 - x0) * step / steps,
                      y0 + (y1 - y0) * step / steps) for step in range(1, steps + 1))
    return dense


def cumulative_relief(hs):
    hs = np.array([h for h in hs if np.isfinite(h)], np.float64)
    if hs.size < 2:
        return 0.0, 0.0
    return float(np.sum(np.abs(np.diff(hs)))), float(hs[-1] - hs[0])
# ===== transmission_path_planner/stations.py =====
"""站点台账与站间线路任务输入。"""

import csv
import hashlib
import math
import re


def route_output_name(route_id):
    """Return a filesystem-safe, collision-resistant directory name for a route ID."""
    route_id = str(route_id)
    stem = re.sub(r"[^A-Za-z0-9_-]+", "_", route_id).strip("_-")[:64] or "route"
    suffix = hashlib.sha256(route_id.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()[:10]
    return f"{stem}-{suffix}"


def station_coordinate_crs_properties(station_task):
    if not station_task:
        return {}
    return {
        "from_station_coordinate_crs": station_task["from_station"]["coordinate_crs"],
        "to_station_coordinate_crs": station_task["to_station"]["coordinate_crs"],
    }


def station_endpoint_properties(station_task):
    if not station_task:
        return {}
    result = {"station_endpoint_qualification": station_task["endpoint_qualification"]}
    for prefix, key in (("from", "from_station"), ("to", "to_station")):
        station = station_task[key]
        result.update({
            f"{prefix}_station_point_role": station["point_role"],
            f"{prefix}_station_verification_status": station["verification_status"],
            f"{prefix}_station_source": station["source"] or None,
            f"{prefix}_station_verification_reference": station["verification_reference"] or None,
        })
    return result


def station_elevation_metrics(station_task):
    """Summarize surveyed station-ground elevations without mixing vertical datums."""
    start = station_task["from_station"]
    end = station_task["to_station"]
    start_elevation = start["ground_elevation_m"]
    end_elevation = end["ground_elevation_m"]
    start_datum = start["vertical_datum"]
    end_datum = end["vertical_datum"]
    if start_elevation is None and end_elevation is None:
        status, delta = "dem_only", None
    elif start_elevation is None or end_elevation is None:
        status, delta = "incomplete_station_measurements", None
    elif not start["ground_elevation_source"] or not end["ground_elevation_source"]:
        status, delta = "elevation_source_missing", None
    elif not start_datum or not end_datum:
        status, delta = "vertical_datum_missing", None
    elif start_datum.casefold() != end_datum.casefold():
        status, delta = "incompatible_vertical_datums", None
    else:
        status = "comparable"
        delta = round(end_elevation - start_elevation, 2)
    return {
        "from_station_ground_elevation_m": start_elevation,
        "from_station_ground_elevation_source": start["ground_elevation_source"] or None,
        "from_station_vertical_datum": start_datum or None,
        "to_station_ground_elevation_m": end_elevation,
        "to_station_ground_elevation_source": end["ground_elevation_source"] or None,
        "to_station_vertical_datum": end_datum or None,
        "station_ground_elevation_status": status,
        "station_ground_elevation_delta_m": delta,
    }


def station_clearance_elevation_overrides(station_task, dem_vertical_datum=None):
    """Return surveyed terminal elevations only when their declared datum matches the DEM."""
    if not station_task:
        return None, "dem_only"
    start = station_task["from_station"]
    end = station_task["to_station"]
    if start["ground_elevation_m"] is None and end["ground_elevation_m"] is None:
        return None, "dem_only"

    metrics = station_elevation_metrics(station_task)
    if metrics["station_ground_elevation_status"] != "comparable":
        return None, metrics["station_ground_elevation_status"]
    if not dem_vertical_datum or not str(dem_vertical_datum).strip():
        return None, "dem_vertical_datum_missing"
    station_datum = start["vertical_datum"].strip()
    if station_datum.casefold() != str(dem_vertical_datum).strip().casefold():
        return None, "incompatible_dem_vertical_datum"
    return {
        "start_ground_elevation_m": start["ground_elevation_m"],
        "goal_ground_elevation_m": end["ground_elevation_m"],
    }, "station_and_dem_vertical_datums_declared_matching"


def _dict_reader(file_obj, label, required_fields):
    reader = csv.DictReader(file_obj)
    fields = reader.fieldnames or []
    if any(not field for field in fields) or len(fields) != len(set(fields)):
        raise ValueError(f"{label}表头存在空列名或重复列名")
    missing = required_fields - set(fields)
    if missing:
        raise ValueError(f"{label}缺少列: {', '.join(sorted(missing))}")
    return reader


def load_station_tasks(stations_csv, route_tasks_csv, allow_unverified=False):
    """读取 stations.csv 与 route_tasks.csv，验证站点引用并返回规划任务。"""
    stations = {}
    with open(stations_csv, encoding="utf-8-sig", newline="") as f:
        reader = _dict_reader(f, "站点", {"station_id", "lat", "lon", "coordinate_crs"})
        for row_number, row in enumerate(reader, start=2):
            if None in row:
                raise ValueError(f"站点表第 {row_number} 行字段数多于表头")
            station_id = (row.get("station_id") or "").strip()
            if not station_id:
                raise ValueError("站点表缺少 station_id")
            if station_id in stations:
                raise ValueError(f"站点 ID 重复: {station_id}")
            coordinate_crs = (row.get("coordinate_crs") or "").strip().upper()
            if coordinate_crs != "EPSG:4326":
                raise ValueError(
                    f"站点 {station_id} 的 coordinate_crs 必须明确为 EPSG:4326；"
                    "其他坐标系请先转换后再填写 lat/lon")
            try:
                lat, lon = float(row["lat"]), float(row["lon"])
            except (KeyError, TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
                raise ValueError(f"站点 {station_id} 的 lat/lon 无效") from exc
            if not (-90 <= lat <= 90 and -180 <= lon <= 180):
                raise ValueError(f"站点 {station_id} 的经纬度超出范围")
            elevation_text = (row.get("ground_elevation_m") or "").strip()
            try:
                ground_elevation = float(elevation_text) if elevation_text else None
            except ValueError as exc:
                raise ValueError(f"站点 {station_id} 的 ground_elevation_m 无效") from exc
            if ground_elevation is not None and not math.isfinite(ground_elevation):
                raise ValueError(f"站点 {station_id} 的 ground_elevation_m 必须是有限数值")
            point_role = (row.get("point_role") or "unknown").strip().casefold()
            verification_status = (row.get("verification_status") or "unknown").strip().casefold()
            if point_role not in {"line_connection_point", "station_centroid", "unknown"}:
                raise ValueError(f"站点 {station_id} 的 point_role 无效")
            if verification_status not in {"verified", "unverified", "unknown"}:
                raise ValueError(f"站点 {station_id} 的 verification_status 无效")
            stations[station_id] = {
                "station_id": station_id,
                "name": (row.get("name") or station_id).strip(),
                "lat": lat,
                "lon": lon,
                "coordinate_crs": coordinate_crs,
                "voltage_kv": (row.get("voltage_kv") or "").strip(),
                "station_type": (row.get("station_type") or "").strip(),
                "source": (row.get("source") or "").strip(),
                "verified": (row.get("verified") or "").strip(),
                "point_role": point_role,
                "verification_status": verification_status,
                "verification_reference": (row.get("verification_reference") or "").strip(),
                "ground_elevation_m": ground_elevation,
                "ground_elevation_source": (row.get("ground_elevation_source") or "").strip(),
                "vertical_datum": (row.get("vertical_datum") or "").strip(),
            }

    tasks = []
    route_ids = set()
    with open(route_tasks_csv, encoding="utf-8-sig", newline="") as f:
        reader = _dict_reader(
            f, "线路任务", {"route_id", "from_station_id", "to_station_id"})
        for row_number, row in enumerate(reader, start=2):
            if None in row:
                raise ValueError(f"线路任务表第 {row_number} 行字段数多于表头")
            route_id = (row.get("route_id") or "").strip()
            from_id = (row.get("from_station_id") or "").strip()
            to_id = (row.get("to_station_id") or "").strip()
            if not route_id or not from_id or not to_id:
                raise ValueError("线路任务表需要 route_id、from_station_id、to_station_id")
            if route_id in route_ids:
                raise ValueError(f"线路任务 ID 重复: {route_id}")
            if from_id == to_id:
                raise ValueError(f"线路任务起终站相同: {route_id}")
            if from_id not in stations or to_id not in stations:
                raise ValueError(f"线路任务 {route_id} 引用了站点表中不存在的 ID")
            start, end = stations[from_id], stations[to_id]
            route_voltage = (row.get("voltage_kv") or "").strip()
            if not route_voltage:
                if start["voltage_kv"] and end["voltage_kv"] and start["voltage_kv"] != end["voltage_kv"]:
                    raise ValueError(f"线路任务 {route_id} 两端电压等级不同，请在任务表填写线路 voltage_kv")
                route_voltage = start["voltage_kv"] or end["voltage_kv"]
            endpoints_declared_verified = all(
                station["point_role"] == "line_connection_point"
                and station["verification_status"] == "verified"
                and station["source"]
                and station["verification_reference"]
                for station in (start, end)
            )
            endpoint_qualification = (
                "declared_verified_line_connection_points"
                if endpoints_declared_verified else "exploratory_only_unverified_endpoints"
            )
            if not endpoints_declared_verified and not allow_unverified:
                raise ValueError(
                    f"线路任务 {route_id} 的两端必须声明为已核验线路接入点，"
                    "并填写 source 与 verification_reference；探索用途可显式使用 "
                    "--allow-unverified-endpoints"
                )
            tasks.append({
                "route_id": route_id,
                "from_station": start,
                "to_station": end,
                "start": (start["lat"], start["lon"]),
                "end": (end["lat"], end["lon"]),
                "voltage_kv": route_voltage,
                "endpoint_qualification": endpoint_qualification,
            })
            route_ids.add(route_id)
    if not tasks:
        raise ValueError("线路任务表为空")
    return tasks
# ===== transmission_path_planner/terrarium.py =====
"""Helpers for correctly locating XYZ Terrarium elevation pixels."""

import math

import numpy as np
from affine import Affine


WEB_MERCATOR_RADIUS_M = 6378137.0
XYZ_TILE_SIZE = 256


def terrarium_rgb_to_elevation(rgb):
    """Decode Terrarium RGB pixels into metres, preserving float fractions."""
    pixels = np.asarray(rgb)
    if pixels.ndim < 3 or pixels.shape[-1] < 3:
        raise ValueError("Terrarium pixels must have at least three RGB channels")
    values = pixels[..., :3].astype(np.float64, copy=False)
    return (values[..., 0] * 256.0 + values[..., 1]
            + values[..., 2] / 256.0 - 32768.0).astype(np.float32)


def xyz_tile_transform(zoom, tile_x, tile_y):
    """Return the source-pixel affine transform in EPSG:3857 for an XYZ tile."""
    scale = XYZ_TILE_SIZE * (2 ** int(zoom))
    resolution = 2.0 * math.pi * WEB_MERCATOR_RADIUS_M / scale
    left = -math.pi * WEB_MERCATOR_RADIUS_M + tile_x * XYZ_TILE_SIZE * resolution
    top = math.pi * WEB_MERCATOR_RADIUS_M - tile_y * XYZ_TILE_SIZE * resolution
    return Affine(resolution, 0.0, left, 0.0, -resolution, top)


def xyz_pixel_to_lonlat(zoom, tile_x, tile_y, pixel_x, pixel_y):
    """Return the WGS84 center of a pixel in a Web Mercator XYZ tile."""
    x, y = xyz_tile_transform(zoom, tile_x, tile_y) * (pixel_x + 0.5, pixel_y + 0.5)
    lon = math.degrees(x / WEB_MERCATOR_RADIUS_M)
    lat = math.degrees(math.atan(math.sinh(y / WEB_MERCATOR_RADIUS_M)))
    return lon, lat
# ===== transmission_path_planner/visualization.py =====
# transmission_path_planner/visualization.py
"""结果可视化（规划路线对比、价值图、指标柱状图）。"""

from .config import *
from .geometry import *
from .data import _hillshade, _fill
from .io_export import *


def _block_mean_for_display(values, factor):
    values = np.asarray(values)
    if factor <= 1:
        return values
    height, width = values.shape
    out_h, out_w = math.ceil(height / factor), math.ceil(width / factor)
    padded = np.pad(
        values,
        ((0, out_h * factor - height), (0, out_w * factor - width)),
        mode="edge",
    )
    return padded.reshape(out_h, factor, out_w, factor).mean(axis=(1, 3))


def _block_max_for_display(mask, factor):
    mask = np.asarray(mask)
    if factor <= 1:
        return mask
    height, width = mask.shape
    out_h, out_w = math.ceil(height / factor), math.ceil(width / factor)
    padded = np.pad(
        mask.astype(np.uint8),
        ((0, out_h * factor - height), (0, out_w * factor - width)),
        mode="constant",
        constant_values=0,
    )
    return padded.reshape(out_h, factor, out_w, factor).max(axis=(1, 3))


def plot_comparison(dem, transform, methods_paths, start_ll, end_ll, out_path, title="规划路线对比",
                    real_ll=None, hazard_masks=None):
    H, W = dem.shape
    left = transform.c; bottom = transform.f + transform.e * H
    right = transform.c + transform.a * W; top = transform.f
    extent = [left, right, bottom, top]
    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(12, 9), dpi=110)
    try:
        factor = max(1, math.ceil(max(H, W) / 1200))
        display_dem = _block_mean_for_display(dem, factor)
        hill = _hillshade(display_dem)
        ax.imshow(hill, extent=extent, cmap="gray", origin="upper", aspect="auto")
        z = _fill(display_dem).astype(np.float32, copy=False)
        ax.imshow(z, extent=extent, cmap="terrain", origin="upper", aspect="auto",
                  alpha=0.30, vmin=np.nanpercentile(z, 2), vmax=np.nanpercentile(z, 98))
        hazard_colors = {
            "debris_flow_impact": ("#f39c12", "土石流影响范围"),
            "landslide_potential": ("#9b59b6", "大规模崩塌潜势区"),
            "landslide_impact": ("#e74c3c", "大规模崩塌影响范围"),
        }
        hazard_legend = None
        if hazard_masks:
            from matplotlib.colors import ListedColormap
            from matplotlib.patches import Patch
            handles = []
            for key, (color, label) in hazard_colors.items():
                mask = hazard_masks.get(key)
                if mask is None:
                    continue
                mask = np.asarray(mask)
                if factor > 1:
                    mask = _block_max_for_display(mask, factor)
                if not mask.any():
                    continue
                ax.imshow(np.ma.masked_where(mask == 0, mask),
                          extent=extent, cmap=ListedColormap([color]),
                          origin="upper", aspect="auto", alpha=0.28,
                          interpolation="nearest", zorder=2)
                handles.append(Patch(facecolor=color, alpha=0.28, label=label))
            if handles:
                hazard_legend = ax.legend(handles=handles, loc="lower left", framealpha=0.85,
                                          title="官方灾害面（软风险）")
        colors = {"VIN-Grad": "#1f77ff", "PPO": "#2ca02c", "A*(baseline)": "#ff7f0e",
                  "A*(weighted)": "#9467bd", "A*(turn-aware)": "#17becf",
                  "A*(8-neighbor exact experimental)": "#8c564b",
                  "A*(16-neighbor experimental)": "#7f7f7f",
                  "A*(8-neighbor shared objective experimental)": "#bcbd22",
                  "A*(16-neighbor shared objective experimental)": "#d62728",
                  "Theta*(experimental)": "#e377c2"}
        line_styles = {
            "A*(baseline)": "-",
            "A*(weighted)": "--",
            "A*(turn-aware)": ":",
            "A*(8-neighbor exact experimental)": "-.",
            "A*(16-neighbor experimental)": (0, (3, 1, 1, 1)),
            "A*(8-neighbor shared objective experimental)": (0, (1, 1)),
            "A*(16-neighbor shared objective experimental)": (0, (5, 2, 1, 2)),
            "Theta*(experimental)": (0, (5, 1)),
        }
        for name, geo in methods_paths.items():
            if not geo:
                continue
            ax.plot([p[0] for p in geo], [p[1] for p in geo],
                    color=colors.get(name, "#555"),
                    linestyle=line_styles.get(name, "-"),
                    linewidth=2.2, label=f"{name} ({path_length_km(geo):.1f}km)", zorder=4)
        if real_ll and len(real_ll) >= 1:
            from matplotlib.collections import LineCollection
            polys = real_ll if isinstance(real_ll[0][0], (list, tuple)) else [real_ll]
            segs = [[(p[0], p[1]) for p in poly] for poly in polys if len(poly) >= 2]
            if segs:
                lc = LineCollection(segs, colors="black", linestyles=":", linewidths=2.2, zorder=6)
                ax.add_collection(lc)
                ax.plot([], [], color="black", linestyle=":", linewidth=2.6,
                        label=f"配对参考线 ({real_length_km(real_ll):.0f}km)", zorder=6)
        if start_ll:
            ax.scatter([start_ll[1]], [start_ll[0]], c="green", s=130, marker="o", edgecolors="k", label="起点", zorder=5)
        if end_ll:
            ax.scatter([end_ll[1]], [end_ll[0]], c="red", s=150, marker="*", edgecolors="k", label="终点", zorder=5)
        ax.set_xlabel("Longitude"); ax.set_ylabel("Latitude")
        ax.set_title(title)
        ax.legend(loc="upper right", framealpha=0.9)
        if hazard_legend:
            ax.add_artist(hazard_legend)
        ax.set_aspect("equal", adjustable="datalim")
        fig.tight_layout()
        fig.savefig(out_path, dpi=110)
    finally:
        plt.close(fig)
    print(f"  对比图保存: {out_path}")


def plot_value_map(V, transform, geo_vin, start_ll, end_ll, out_path):
    H, W = V.shape
    left = transform.c; bottom = transform.f + transform.e * H
    right = transform.c + transform.a * W; top = transform.f
    extent = [left, right, bottom, top]
    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(11, 9), dpi=160)
    vmin, vmax = np.nanpercentile(V, 1), np.nanpercentile(V, 99)
    im = ax.imshow(V, extent=extent, cmap="viridis", origin="upper", aspect="auto", vmin=vmin, vmax=vmax)
    if geo_vin:
        ax.plot([p[0] for p in geo_vin], [p[1] for p in geo_vin], color="white", linewidth=1.6, zorder=4)
    if start_ll:
        ax.scatter([start_ll[1]], [start_ll[0]], c="lime", s=120, marker="o", edgecolors="k", zorder=5)
    if end_ll:
        ax.scatter([end_ll[1]], [end_ll[0]], c="red", s=150, marker="*", edgecolors="k", zorder=5)
    ax.set_title("神经值传播概率图 V(s) 与梯度追踪路径")
    ax.set_xlabel("Longitude"); ax.set_ylabel("Latitude")
    ax.set_aspect("equal", adjustable="datalim")
    fig.colorbar(im, ax=ax, fraction=0.046, pad=0.04, label="值(代价-到此为止)")
    fig.tight_layout(); fig.savefig(out_path, dpi=160); plt.close(fig)
    print(f"  值传播概率图保存: {out_path}")


_COMPARISON_METHODS = [
    "VIN-Grad", "PPO", "A*(baseline)", "A*(weighted)", "A*(turn-aware)",
    "A*(8-neighbor exact experimental)", "A*(16-neighbor experimental)",
    "A*(8-neighbor shared objective experimental)",
    "A*(16-neighbor shared objective experimental)",
    "Theta*(experimental)",
]
_COMPARISON_METRICS = [
    "length_km", "mean_cost", "sinuosity", "hard_violations", "time_s", "cum_relief_m",
]


def _algo_comparison_data(all_results):
    methods = [m for m in _COMPARISON_METHODS
               if any(m in rec["metrics"] for rec in all_results)]
    agg = {m: {k: [] for k in _COMPARISON_METRICS} for m in methods}
    for rec in all_results:
        for m, met in rec["metrics"].items():
            if m not in agg:   # 跳过最终路线记录等非量化对比条目
                continue
            for k in _COMPARISON_METRICS:
                if (k == "hard_violations"
                        and met.get("hard_constraint_status") == "no_path"):
                    continue
                value = met.get(k)
                if value is not None and isinstance(value, (int, float)) and math.isfinite(value):
                    agg[m][k].append(value)
    return methods, agg


def plot_algo_comparison(all_results, out_path):
    """多算法指标聚合柱状图。"""
    methods, agg = _algo_comparison_data(all_results)
    if not methods:
        return
    metrics_keys = _COMPARISON_METRICS
    means = {m: {k: (sum(v) / len(v) if v else None)
                 for k, v in agg[m].items()} for m in methods}
    short_names = {
        "A*(baseline)": "A* 基线", "A*(weighted)": "A* 加权",
        "A*(turn-aware)": "A* 转向代价", "A*(8-neighbor exact experimental)": "A* 8邻域精确",
        "A*(16-neighbor experimental)": "A* 16邻域", "Theta*(experimental)": "Theta*",
        "A*(8-neighbor shared objective experimental)": "A* 8邻域共目标",
        "A*(16-neighbor shared objective experimental)": "A* 16邻域共目标",
        "VIN-Grad": "VIN-Grad", "PPO": "PPO",
    }
    labels = {
        "length_km": "路径长度 (km)", "mean_cost": "平均相对代价",
        "sinuosity": "弯曲度", "hard_violations": "硬约束违规(点)",
        "time_s": "路径生成总耗时 (s)", "cum_relief_m": "累计高差 (m)"}
    fig, axes = plt.subplots(2, 3, figsize=(15, 9), dpi=140)
    axes = axes.ravel()
    colors = {"VIN-Grad": "#1f77ff", "PPO": "#2ca02c", "A*(baseline)": "#ff7f0e",
              "A*(weighted)": "#9467bd", "A*(turn-aware)": "#17becf",
              "A*(8-neighbor exact experimental)": "#8c564b",
              "A*(16-neighbor experimental)": "#7f7f7f",
              "A*(8-neighbor shared objective experimental)": "#bcbd22",
              "A*(16-neighbor shared objective experimental)": "#d62728",
              "Theta*(experimental)": "#e377c2"}
    for i, k in enumerate(metrics_keys):
        ax = axes[i]
        vals = [means[m][k] for m in methods]
        positions = list(range(len(methods)))
        for position, method, value in zip(positions, methods, vals):
            if value is None:
                ax.text(position, 0.02, "N/A", transform=ax.get_xaxis_transform(),
                        ha="center", va="bottom", fontsize=9)
            else:
                bar = ax.bar(position, value, color=colors[method])[0]
                ax.text(bar.get_x() + bar.get_width() / 2, value,
                        f"{value:.2f}", ha="center", va="bottom", fontsize=8)
        ax.set_title(labels[k])
        ax.set_ylabel(labels[k])
        tick_labels = [
            f"{short_names[m]}\nN={len(agg[m][k])}"
            if means[m][k] is not None else f"{short_names[m]}\nN/A"
            for m in methods
        ]
        ax.set_xticks(positions, tick_labels, fontsize=8)
        ax.margins(y=0.16)
    fig.suptitle("多算法路径规划量化对比（各走廊均值）", fontsize=14)
    fig.tight_layout(rect=[0, 0, 1, 0.96])
    try:
        fig.savefig(out_path, dpi=140)
    except Exception:
        import time as _time
        try:
            _time.sleep(0.2)
            _alt = out_path.with_name(out_path.stem + "_1" + out_path.suffix)
            fig.savefig(_alt, dpi=140)
            out_path = _alt
        except Exception as _se:
            plt.close(fig)
            print(f"  [WARN] 指标对比图保存失败(已跳过): {_se}")
            return
    plt.close(fig)
    print(f"  指标对比图保存: {out_path}")
